*When one becomes serious to follow the mission of the spiritual master, his resolution is tantamount to seeing the Supreme Personality of Godhead.* As explained before, this means meeting the Supreme Personality of Godhead in the instruction of the spiritual master. This is technically called *vāṇī-seva*. Śrīla Viśvanātha Cakravartī Ṭhākura states in his *Bhagavad-gītā* commentary on the verse *vyavasāyātmikā buddhir ekeha kuru-nandana* (*Bg*. 2.41) that one should serve the words of the spiritual master. The disciple must stick to whatever the spiritual master orders. *Simply by following on that line, one sees the Supreme Personality of Godhead.* \[*Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 4.28.51 purport; emphasis added\]

Viśvanātha Cakravartī Ṭhākura has taught very, very nicely about *guru*. Therefore he has written in *Gurv-aṣṭaka*, *yasya prasādād bhagavat-prasādaḥ*. He is example, practical example of *guru-bhakti*, Viśvanātha Cakravartī Ṭhākura. He accepted his guru, Narottama dāsa Ṭhākura. *So he said that “I am not interested for my salvation or going back to Godhead. I am not interested.”“Interested” means it may come, it may not come. “That I don’t mind. But I am interested only with the words of my guru.” Viśvanātha Cakravartī said. “That is my life. Whether I will be successful or not successful, it doesn’t matter. I must take the words of my Guru Mahārāja as my life and soul.”*\[*Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* lecture, April 1976, Bombay; emphasis added\]

You describe how in Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura’s final days he confirmed his original orders to you in a letter:

I wrote him a letter, “My dear master, your other disciples, *brahmacārī*, *sannyāsī*, they are rendering you direct service. And I am a householder. I cannot live with you, I cannot serve you nicely. So I do not know. How can I serve you?” Simply an idea, I was thinking of serving him, “How can I serve him seriously?” So the reply was dated 13th December, 1936. In that letter he wrote, “My dear such and such, I am very glad to receive your letter. I think you should try to push our movement in English.” That was his writing. “And that will do good to you and to the people who will help you.” That was his instruction. And then in 1936, on the 31st December—that means just after writing this letter, a fortnight before his departure—he passed away. But I took that order of my spiritual master very seriously. . . . If we strictly try to serve the spiritual master, his order, then Kṛṣṇa will give us all facilities. That is the secret. Although there was no possibility, I never thought, but I took it little seriously by studying a commentary by Viśvanātha Cakravartī Ṭhākura on the *Bhagavad-gītā*. In the *Bhagavad-gītā* the verse *vyavasāyātmikā-buddhir ekeha kuru-nandana,* in connection with that verse, *Viśvanātha Cakravartī Ṭhākura gives his commentary that we should take up the words from the spiritual master as our life and soul. We should try to carry out the instruction, the specific instruction of the spiritual master, very rigidly, without caring for our personal benefit or loss.* \[Disappearance Day Lecture, December 1968, Los Angeles; emphasis added\]

In your Vyāsa-pūjā edition of *Back to Godhead* of 1944, you listed a few more details of the reply written to you by Śrīla Sarasvatī Ṭhākura:

In the course of writing that letter, He \[Śrīla Sarasvatī Ṭhākura\] wrote the following lines amongst other things: “I am fully confident that you can explain in English our thoughts and arguments to the people who are not conversant with the languages of other members. . . . This will do much good to yourself as well as your audience. . . . I have every hope that you can turn yourself \[into\] a very good English preacher if you serve the mission to inculcate the novel impression to the people in general and philosophers of modern age and religiosity.”

Śrīla Prabhupāda, sometimes we misunderstand, or have misunderstood, your unparalleled empowerment, authority, and vision, as well as your unique and fully conceived presentation of the philosophy of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, specifically tailored to a worldwide preaching movement. Your realized vision and perspective about how to present Kṛṣṇa consciousness (especially to the Western world—indeed, everywhere in the world!) needs to be always uppermost in the minds of your followers, particularly when disruptive and alien ideas are being propagated by persons either inimical to, or uncomprehending of, your unique perspective, bestowed upon you by the previous *acaryas*. There are many elevated Vaiṣṇavas, but clearly none have been gifted with such a universal vision of Kṛṣṇa consciousness as you. The proof is that no one before or since has even come close to duplicating your feats of coming to the West in a penniless and friendless condition and establishing a worldwide preaching movement of unsurpassed dynamism and effectiveness. (This does not excuse subsequent lackluster efforts or deviations by us, your disciples and granddisciples, but refers to the unique preaching movement created by you, still very much alive, active, and dynamic all over the world by the efforts of your sincere followers.)

Therefore, *your* opinions regarding the points of philosophical emphasis, preaching tactics, and priorities of concern must be given the first and foremost position in the hearts of those purporting to be your followers. After all, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the one whom Lord Caitanya and Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura, as well as the predecessor *ācāryas* like Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī, personally empowered to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness throughout the entire world. No one else has shown such a miraculous ability to present the universal essence of the teachings of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu and Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī to all countries and cultures of the world in a form which can be embraced and understood by all. None of your godbrothers or godnephews has even come close to such an achievement, despite their other many exalted qualities.

Some devotees question that since there are many branches and subbranches of Gauḍīya Vaiṣṇavism, why should we make any discrimination between your presentation of Kṛṣṇa consciousness and the presentations of other Gauḍīya Vaiṣṇavas who are also preaching Kṛṣṇa consciousness? Isn’t Gauḍīya Vaiṣṇavism simply the teachings of Lord Caitanya and the Six Gosvāmīs? They argue, “Why should we quibble over minor differences?” Elements of truth are touched on in such questions. ISKCON devotees do risk being charged with fanaticism or sectarianism by being too rigid in their views, and also run the risk of making offenses if they are dismissive of other Vaiṣṇavas. At the same time, there are inherent defects in thinking that making discriminations about whom to listen to amongst the Gauḍīya Vaiṣṇavas is wrong or offensive. In the first place, some persons claiming to represent the conclusions of the previous *ācāryas* only seem to be doing so when in fact they are presenting alien and imported ideas which are *apasiddhānta* (against the proper conclusion) or even *sahajiyā* (sentimental or imaginary), which either deviate from or cheapen, respectively, the line of thought descending from Rūpa Gosvāmī down to Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura and you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Even when it is unclear if *apasiddhānta* or *sahajiyā* conclusions are being presented, certain Vaiṣṇavas still tend to emphasize personal spiritual advancement, *rāgānuga-bhakti*, and the premature relishing of Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa *līlā*. These practices deviate from the line of emphasis of the two Prabhupādas (Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura Prabhupāda and you, Śrīla Prabhupāda), and are placed in higher priority than service to the *saṅkīrtana* movement. Your emphasis, your order, was practical service to *saṅkīrtana* in the form of large-scale distribution of *your* translations and commentaries of the Gauḍīya Vaiṣṇava literature, *harināma-saṅkīrtana*, *prasādam* distribution, festivals, and opening centers everywhere. If the teachings of certain Vaiṣṇavas serve to diminish, weaken, or distract from such efforts, then those teachings and those Vaiṣṇavas actually perform a disservice to your mission, which strictly adheres to the line of Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura and Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī.

Therefore, we must try to enter deeply into an understanding of your mission of true compassion, and, with an understanding of the unique character of your mission strongly established, we will be able to please you. By concoction and imagination we can never be successful. You stressed again and again that you had given us all that we needed in your books and teachings. You also emphasized repeatedly that we should not change anything and simply present what we had heard from you and should execute the programs you had designed for us. You guaranteed that such following would lead to the perfection of the human form of life and that we would return back home, back to Godhead. In the following excerpt you clearly state your mission in your own wonderful style:

So the Gosvāmīs have given their principles, especially for \[the\] general public in the *Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu*. We have translated this book into *Nectar of Devotion*. So our business is . . . everything is there, we have to follow. And my Guru Mahārāja is described as *rūpānuga-viruddha-apasiddhānta-dhvānta-hāriṇe*. He strictly followed the *rūpānuga* principle. *Rūpānuga* principle, Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī directs in the *Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu*, “You somehow or other engage people in Kṛṣṇa consciousness.”*Yena tena prakāreṇa manaḥ kṛṣṇe niveśayet, sarve vidhi-niṣedhā syur etayor eva kiṅkarāḥ*. Just like in Europe and America, some of our friends criticize that “Swami Mahārāja, he is not doing this, not doing that.” We are doing everything, but our main business is just to induce them to Kṛṣṇa consciousness. That is the secret. You have to induce them. Then even there are some discrepancies in the rules and regulations, it does not matter. Rūpa Gosvāmī says, *sarve vidhi-niṣedhā syur etayor eva kiṅkarāḥ*. First of all, we must be Kṛṣṇa conscious somehow or other. We must find out. It can be changed according to country, climate, and circumstances. But the result, we have to see by the result whether one has become Kṛṣṇa conscious. That is wanted*.* That is wanted. Then we say that this is the rule: you don’t do this, we don’t do this. In the beginning we have many difficulties. Because their behavior, their culture, everything \[is\] just opposite. Just opposite. How you can expect \[that\] everything \[is\] to the right point? That is not possible. So we have to find out means how he becomes Kṛṣṇa conscious. That is my first business. So that is the secret.

So Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta SarasvatīGosvāmī followed strictly Rūpa Gosvāmī. Just like Bhaktisiddhānta SarasvatīGosvāmī introduced that a *sannyāsī* should go for begging alms in motorcar. In motorcar! They should live in palatial buildings. Now if in this age if we follow that *rūpānuga* . . . Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī used to live underneath a tree, and if we say now we shall also imitate . . . , just like there are many *bābājīs* in Vṛndāvana, they are imitating, a small loincloth but doing all nonsense. So imitation is not required. Real thing is required. How, what is the purpose of Gosvāmī? They say that *yena tena prakāreṇa manaḥ kṛṣṇe*. Somehow or other first of all engage him in Kṛṣṇa consciousness. That is my Guru Mahārāja’s gift. He first of all started that there is no need of going to Vṛndāvana and imitate the Gosvāmīs. Live in big, big cities, in big, big palatial houses, but preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness. People may understand. Give chance to the people how they will understand Kṛṣṇa consciousness. . . .

He \[Śrīla Sarasvatī Ṭhākura\] opened many centers. Similarly, in big, big cities. So we are doing that. And because we are following strictly the principles of Rūpa Gosvāmī, *rūpānuga*, therefore it is somehow or other coming out very successful. The Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement should be pushed according to the *rūpānuga* principle. \[*Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* lecture, March 1972, Calcutta\]

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have given us everything, and you have given us the perfect example of how to follow the *ācārya*. The specific program and methods for preaching Kṛṣṇa consciousness and achieving the goal of human life have been given by you. There is no necessity of additions or deletions. If we follow you, we will have no problems, and we can also become fearless at the lotus feet of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu by your mercy. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Your servants at Bhaktivedanta Manor, UK.  
(written by Rūpa Vilāsa Dāsa)

## Boston

Our dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

We at New Guṇḍicā*dhāma* are ever grateful to be entrusted with carrying on your mission here. It was here in Boston, at Commonwealth Pier, that you first landed in the U.S., on September 17th, 1965. As we look out at that pier, where you first blessed this country with the dust of your lotus feet, we remember the poem you wrote upon your arrival, entitled “Mārkine Bhāgavata-dharma,” and we are simultaneously humbled and inspired. Some of the verses you wrote on board the ship that day are as follows:

My dear Lord Kṛṣṇa, You are so kind upon this useless soul, but I do not know why You have brought me here. Now You can do whatever You like with me. . . .

O Lord, I am just like a puppet in Your hands. So if You have brought me here to dance, then make me dance, make me dance, O Lord, make me dance as You like. . . .

I have no devotion, nor do I have any knowledge, but I have strong faith in the holy name of Kṛṣṇa. I have been designated as Bhaktivedanta, and now, if You like, You can fulfill the real purport of Bhaktivedanta.

(Signed—the most unfortunate, insignificant beggar, A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami, on board the ship *Jaladuta,* Commonwealth Pier, Boston, Massachusetts, U.S.A. Dated 18th September 1965)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, what devotion to Kṛṣṇa or knowledge of Him do we have? What do we have of value in this world? We have only your mercy and our faith in Your Divine Grace. In truth, our faith in you is all we are made of.

We feel your presence and your care for us every day, and we know that you are watching over and guiding us. A child gains strength from the love of the father and mother. Similarly, despite our shortcomings and all that Māyā throws at us, we remain strong in your love for us.

The lasting legacy of your love carries on in the books you have written. You said that you put the best of yourself in your books. Please help us understand the purport of your books and chant Kṛṣṇa’s names with attention. Thus we can become ambassadors of Lord Caitanya’s movement and make your temple a beacon of knowledge within the city of Boston.

Our dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, please help us see ourselves the way you see us. Please help us envision our potential the way you envision it. Please help us chalk out the preaching for the Boston *yātrā* the way you chalked it out: boldly, resourcefully, with purity, with enthusiasm, and with ingenuity. Please help us maintain the integrity of the Vaiṣṇava lineage you have entrusted to us.

Our dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, please help us acquire humility so that we may steadily strive to always be situated in your transcendental service and association.

Begging to be your humble servants, we remain

Your devotees in Boston, Massachusetts, USA.  
(written by Saṅkīrtana Dāsī)

## Brahmapur

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

By your mercy, at present we are building a *satsaṅga bhavān,* where more people can sit properly to hear Kṛṣṇa’s original message as it is, as per your guidelines, and where more of your books can be distributed. The remaining work on the *āśrama* and our other projects is, slowly but surely, progressing well. We are trying our best to complete the boundary wall and main gate before the end of this year.

We made our morning program very strict to enable our *brahmacārīs* to complete at least sixteen rounds of the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra* before seven in the morning every day. Then they can go out with enthusiasm to distribute more books.

The devotees of ISKCON Brahmapur, India.

## Brampton/Mississauga

Our glorious father Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our unlimited *daṇḍavats* at your lotus feet. All glories to your divine personality!

Śukadeva Gosvāmī said: Then, O King, religion, truthfulness, cleanliness, tolerance, mercy, duration of life, physical strength, and memory will all diminish day by day because of the powerful influence of the Age of Kali. \[*Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 12.2.1\]

This is just the first of many verses describing the qualities of Kali-yuga. As each day passes, we are able to see more and more of the different symptoms of this Age of Kali. But in a world being overrun by corruption, deceit, godlessness, and unhappiness, you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, have left behind a legacy of spirituality, honesty, hope, and happiness.

In 1922 your spiritual master instructed you “to preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness to the English-speaking world.” Taking this instruction as your life and soul, you accepted many hardships by coming to the West, writing so many books for our benefit, opening temples and preaching centers, spreading the *saṅkīrtana* movement, and giving us the best opportunity to live life on the spiritual platform. The philosophy you brought is the remedy for this advancing Age of Kali, for it shows us our real, eternal position as servants of God. This realization allows us to rise above the material platform and be truly happy.

You are the model of compassion, for you have taught fallen souls like us the way back to Godhead. You are a *mahā-bhāgavata* who have given us directions how to go back to our Supreme Father. Only with your blessings and your teachings do we have the opportunity to be a part of Lord Caitanya’s movement.

Just as you took your Guru Mahārāja’s instructions as your very life and soul, we pray that we are able to take your instructions as ours. Spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness was your topmost priority, and we pray that at ISKCON Brampton-Mississauga we are able to continue with your mission. You built a house in which the whole world can live, and now, in your honor, it is our duty to make sure that all the rooms are filled. It is not simply being Kṛṣṇa conscious but spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness that will please you, and make our lives successful.

As we work in this material world, performing our daily duties, we beg you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, to remain with us in our homes, in our lives, and in our prayers. Please hear our prayer on this auspicious occasion of your appearance day. We humbly pray, with straws between our teeth, for your blessings so that we can assist you in fulfilling the mission of Lord Caitanya. Thank you for the greatest benediction, which is the opportunity to render service within ISKCON. Hare Kṛṣṇa.

Your eternally indebted servants at ISKCON Brampton/Mississauga, Canada.

## Cakra Lutotin

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

You mercifully appeared in this world to do what no one had ever done before you and no one will ever do afterwards (because you have already done it): spread the mercy of Lord Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu all over this planet. Although some persons tried to put themselves above you after you had departed, your sun still shines and gives guidance to millions. We, your insignificant servants here in Moravia, want to be among those millions. We never met you physically, but we can meet some of your disciples, who sincerely try to present your teachings to others.

You came to us in the form of your books and your devotees. This is our great fortune. We can read the books you wrote and those written about you, we can hear your conversations, read your letters, watch the rare videos with scenes from your life of preaching, and in this way we can be inspired to surrender our lives to your mission. We can do this only by surrendering to your representatives and thus maintaining the principle of *guru-paramparā,* which you stress so much throughout all your books.

You gave us a complete process for attaining the goal, which is to become Kṛṣṇa conscious and spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness everywhere. You taught the message of Lord Caitanya, and you gave us all we need to spread it further in this world. We have to act now, to continue to practice and to spread what you have given us: pure Kṛṣṇa consciousness without any adulteration.

Please keep us always fixed on the track of your ISKCON—humble, submissive, with a good service attitude, together with all the other devotees—so that we can be a valid part of this *saṅkīrtana* movement of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

Your servants in Cakra Lutotin, Czech Republic.  
(written by Aśvahari Dāsa)

## Cali

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances and respect to your divine lotus feet.

In the fight for the material existence, by the mercy of Lord Kṛṣṇa and the spiritual master, we can encounter something that will make us wake up. For us, you came in the form of your books. We appreciate them so much because we find comfort in our struggles in this material nature. Your books inspire us not to give up and fill us with true spiritual pleasure and happiness, which we can’t find out there in the material world. For your glorious books we glorify you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Because your ISKCON family is the only refuge in this world, we come together to worship you and Lord Kṛṣṇa; in this way we enable ourselves and our children to understand the real value of life.

We worship you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, because like children walking hand in hand with their father, we walk in this world in complete trust of your teachings, which you imparted only to help us go back home to Kṛṣṇa. This is the goal you taught was worth fighting for in life.

We worship you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, because every day when receive your teachings, the real meaning of life becomes clear, and therefore every day we see more clearly that we should glorify you as our eternal guru, life after life.

We worship you, Śrīla Prabhupāda . . .

Śrīla Prabhupāda, all glories to your divine lotus feet!

Your servants in Cali, Colombia.

## Cameroon

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our unworthy obeisances to your divine lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

Your mission here in Cameroon, on the central West African coast, has been progressing nicely for the past three years. Though our problems are not as serious as those in other African countries, we still have our difficulties, which we hope we will be able to overcome by your mercy. The devotees in this country are all grateful for your divine blessings, which they were fortunate to receive through your divine books.

With folded hands we beg Your Divine Grace to please bestow your mercy on this country so that we may meet many more favorable souls and attract them to Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, your books are going everywhere in this country. We humbly pray for your blessings that the books will keep going out in this way, and we also beg your mercy toward all devotees so that their parents will stop discouraging them from taking part in this glorious movement, which Your Divine Grace brought to us all.

We pray for more blissful and progressive Kṛṣṇa consciousness here in Cameroon.

Your servants in Cameroon, Central Africa.  
(written by Kuśa Dāsa)

## Canberra

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

We, your disciples and granddisciples at the Canberra temple, bow down together at your lotus feet to offer you our most humble obeisances.

As stated in the *Caitanya-caritāmṛta* (*Ādi-līlā* 1.45),

*guru kṛṣṇa-rūpa hana śāstrera pramāṇe*  
*guru-rūpe kṛṣṇa kṛpā karena bhakta-gaṇe*

**“According to the deliberate opinion of all revealed scriptures, the spiritual master is nondifferent from Kṛṣṇa. Lord Kṛṣṇa in the form of the spiritual master delivers His devotees.”**

Indeed, you appeared in this material world to show us the devotional path back to Lord Kṛṣṇa. We are most grateful to you for the precious gifts you have given to deliver the whole world, such as the holy name, Deity worship, temples, your books, your disciples, and your granddisciples.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, the Canberra *yātrā* has gone through some really difficult times in recent years. However, it is because of your blessings in the form of your recorded instructions, your books, the holy names, *prasādam,* etc., that we are again able to make little steps in fulfilling your mission of spreading *saṅkīrtana* movement of Lord Caitanya.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, in addition to distributing *kṛṣṇa-prasādam* to Canberra and adjoining communities through our Food for Life program twice a week, annual local community festivals and Sunday Feasts, we are also now distributing your books and preaching through our Tuesday Bhakti Vṛkṣa program and Saturday Sādhana and Training program. We are also trying to improve and maintain nice Deity worship for the pleasure of Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Jagannātha, Baladeva, and Subhadrā and Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi. We have also built a big Tulasī house for the pleasure of Tulasī Mahārāṇī. We now have profusely growing Tulasī plants in the glass house, enabling us to incorporate Tulasī leaves and *mañjarīs* in garlands for the pleasure of their Lordships. We are also holding regular *harināmas* and yearly Ratha-yātrās in Canberra. Last year we started a Jagannātha Youth Group, and this is going quite nicely.

These are very small steps, but we hope you are pleased with our efforts to spread your mission. We would like to improve these services further, and we hope you will empower us to render devotional service according to the very high standard you set, in a selfless manner.

Personally, I consider myself very fortunate to be able to engage in Kṛṣṇa conscious activities. This would not have been possible for me or anyone else in the Western world now aspiring to be Kṛṣṇa conscious without your dedicated and selfless efforts to build temples all over the world and to tirelessly write and print so many devotional books. Nothing could stop you from carrying out the instructions of your Guru Mahārāja and Lord Kṛṣṇa. You were absolutely fearless in executing your duties. From doing my devotional service at this small temple, I can understand more clearly how pure and powerful you are. If I have so much difficulty dealing with the many problems that arise from managing this small temple, how enormous must have been the power you possessed to manage so many temples around the world! I can clearly understand that only a pure devotee empowered by the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Lord Kṛṣṇa Himself, could have done that wonderful task. You are the perfect example of determination, steadiness, knowledge, and compassion.

Thank you for the strength I have been receiving from you to overcome the difficulties in my service. Please forgive me and all your other disciples and granddisciples here in Canberra for our shortcomings, mistakes, and offenses. We also beg you for your blessings and guidance so we can become actual devotees, properly following in your footsteps while spreading your love and raising Kṛṣṇa conscious devotees with the same determination, dedication, and love you showed.

Your insignificant servants at Hare Krishna Ashram in Canberra, Australia.  
(written by Raṅganātha Dāsa)

## Caracas

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

My beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda, I remember you with such fond appreciation. On this anniversary of your majestic appearance on our planet, the devotees in Caracas (the place where you personally graced us with a beautiful visit in February of 1975), we want to express to you our deepest feelings of affection, sincerity, and love for the beautiful gesture you made in coming here, undergoing great austerity to teach us of the beauty of *bhakti-yoga*.

We must say very frankly, Śrīla Prabhupāda, that even thirty-four years after your transcendental visit, many of us still do not know what our true responsibilities should be to you. One thing is for sure, though, and that is your order for us to dedicate ourselves to the distribution of your books. Still, it gives me pain to tell you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, that we have not been able to fulfill this important order. Also, our country is an administrative disaster, and a great part of this disorder must be due to not complying with your blessed instruction. Some of us still remember with sadness the beautiful days of *saṅkīrtana,* days that were glorious for our *yātrā* as well as for our beloved devotees, who were then considered *mahā-rathīs* of *saṅkīrtana* worldwide.

But, dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, what we may be bold enough to utter before you with great sincerity is that not all is finished for the Venezuelan *yātrā.* We declare that we will continue the fight to see a future of serious *saṅkīrtana;* indeed, we will fight with such maturity and determination that Kṛṣṇa consciousness will be undeniably stabilized in Venezuela and all of Latin America.

Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we know that out of your infinite mercy and compassion for us you will hear our pleading prayers: Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, give us your great power so that we can continue introducing your valuable books, especially in the universities and all the political, cultural, and social venues of the country. We especially want to introduce them to the leaders, because that was your intense desire. We hope and believe that you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, will pray to Parama Karuṇa Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi (as you lovingly named them), who are our dear Deities, so that They will wake up our hearts. Then this feeling of urgency to introduce your books everywhere will return to all the devotees, and ultimately everyone in the world will become Krishna conscious.

*Jaya* Śrīla Prabhupāda! You are the savior of all the fallen souls of this planet. Please give us mercy and allow us to execute Śrī Kṛṣṇa *saṅkīrtana*. Please give us shade under your lotus feet. Śrīla Prabhupāda-*kī jaya*!

Your servants in Caracas, Venezuela.  
(written by Hari-cakra Dāsa)

## Chandigarh

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Most respected Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet. We feel highly privileged to say a few words on this most auspicious day.

While contemplating Lord Kṛṣṇa’s compassion toward the conditioned living entities, we find how loving He is. Kṛṣṇa accompanies each of them as the Supersoul so that He can guide them in their wanderings in the material world and enlighten them if they again turn their face toward Him. In addition, Kṛṣṇa Himself sometimes comes to this world, either in His original form or as one of His many incarnations, and enacts wonderful pastimes to attract the fallen souls toward Him. Finally, He expresses His affection for all of us by adventing as Lord Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu to propagate the *yuga-dharma,* i.e., *harināma saṅkīrtana,* and thus to munificently distribute *kṛṣṇa-prema* to one and all. Being a pure devotee and close associate of the Lord, you know His heart very well, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

In *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* (9.4.68), Lord Viṣṇu tells Durvāsā Muni:

*sādhavo hṛdayaṁ mahyaṁ sādhūnāṁ hṛdayaṁ tv aham*  
*mad-anyat te na jānanti nāhaṁ tebhyo manāg api*

**“The devotees are always in My heart, and I am always in the hearts of the devotees. The devotee does not know anything beyond Me, and I also cannot forget the devotee. There is a very intimate relationship between Me and the pure devotees. Pure devotees in full knowledge are never out of spiritual touch, and therefore they are very much dear to Me.” (translation quoted from** ***Bhagavad-gītā*** **7.18, purport)**

You know that Lord Caitanya wanted the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra* and *kṛṣṇa-prema* to be carried beyond the boundaries of Bharata. Endowed with the compassion and mercy of Lord Caitanya, you advented 113 years ago this day to spread the message of Lord Caitanya all over the world. And within a short span of twelve years after you sanctified the land of the USA with your lotus feet, Your Divine Grace had laid a very solid foundation for the continued worldwide propagation of Kṛṣṇa consciousness for the next ten thousand years. During this twelve-year period, you gave us over eighty books with your Bhaktivedanta purports, which will carry on your legacy for the coming generations. You taught us by your own example, Śrīla Prabhupāda, that rendering pure devotional service to Lord Kṛṣṇa is the only way to attain a perfectly blissful state of life.

To establish that the Vedic instructions are not merely theoretical, you personally trained your disciples in practical *sādhana* and presented them as living examples of the Vedic spiritual science. You made them into perfect Vaiṣṇavas and gave us the association of such exalted souls so that we could get inspiration and guidance from them. When we see the preaching enthusiasm of your senior disciples who are in advanced age, we feel highly inspired. You have fully empowered your disciples and granddisciples, Śrīla Prabhupāda, to carry on the Hare Kṛṣṇa movement for thousands of years.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, as an exhibition of your divine compassion, you are engaging us in your service, which is the most precious privilege in this creation. As the servants of your servants, we here in Chandigarh have, by your mercy, organized various big festivals like Janmāṣṭamī, Rādhāṣṭamī, Ratha-yātrā, and Gaura Pūrṇimā to distribute Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s mercy to thousands of people. In this we are simply following your perfect example, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

The divine message of the Supreme Lord revealed by your divine *vāṇī* in the form of your books is profusely being distributed, as per your desire. This year, by the blessings of His Holiness Gopāl Krishna Gosvāmī, we were once again able to distribute more than 20,000 *Bhagavad-gītās* in December, the month of the marathon.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, by seeing the exemplary behavior of your exalted disciples who regularly visit us, many young engineers, doctors, and other professionals have started practicing Kṛṣṇa consciousness regularly. Three of them have completely surrendered their lives to the cause of your movement. It is only by your mercy that they have taken this bold step toward your beloved Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

By your special mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we had the good fortune of receiving your scientist disciple His Grace Drutakarmā Prabhu in Chandigarh this year. He gave a talk entitled “Human Devolution: A Vedic Alternative to Darwin’s Theory” to engineers, technologists, scientists, medical students, and teachers in five institutes in our preaching area. His presentation was well received and appreciated.

You will be pleased to hear, Śrīla Prabhupāda, that preaching to the youth and children is going on nicely in Chandigarh. Our young educated *brahmacārīs* are preaching in three engineering colleges and Punjab University, apart from having regular programs for youth in the temple. We have opened one BACE in Chandigarh, close to the engineering college, and are having good response from engineering students.

For young children, we are holding five weekly Bhakta Prahlad classes—one in the Chandigarh temple and four in devotees’ homes nearby. We also held four summer camps for schoolchildren during their summer vacations, each two weeks long, where, along with other Vedic cultural aspects, they were exposed to the basics of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Through these young children, many parents are also taking an interest in Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Our ISKCON youth forum for girls is also doing fine, Śrīla Prabhupāda, by your mercy.

Our preaching to the congregation is also going well, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Over eighty congregational members took *harināma dīkṣā* last year, thus increasing the strength of your spiritual family.

Finally, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we would like to thank you once again for making your compassion available to us in the form of your own exemplary behavior, the divine instructions in your supernatural books, and your great followers. We humbly beg for a little mercy and strength to take advantage of all these transcendental gifts so that we can contribute our best to your movement.

Your servants at ISKCON Chandigarh, India.

## Coimbatore

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our respectful and humble obeisances at your merciful lotus feet. All glories to your presence in this material world! All glories to your devotional service to Lord Caitanya and the Gauḍīya Vaiṣṇava *paramparā,* the previous *ācāryas*.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the leader of all the preachers. Everyone else is following you simply by virtue of the massive contributions you have made to saving living entities in this universe. Śrīla Prabhupāda, your books are everything, as you rightly said. We can see you through your purports constantly. Distributing your books, studying them, preaching their contents, and helping others follow them is the sum and substance of your mission.

From the writings of His Holiness Bhakti Vikāsa Swami, we gather that Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura visited Coimbatore and Ooty on his preaching tours and wrote books while staying here for several months. Here in Coimbatore we have gathered together to dedicate ourselves to your mission. The Bhaktivedanta Academy that was started here has facilitated students who have taken the Bhakti-śāstrī courses and other seminars, enriching their spiritual lives. We plan to merge into the educational ethos of this place and make a mark academically with your books. The signs are very promising. Your blessing is all that is needed.

Simultaneously, we plan to set up an educational complex, with a museum and other facilities, for anyone who wants to become educated in your books. With your blessings, Śrīla Prabhupāda, this can happen and change the future of many conditioned souls. Our eight-acre campus will also house a temple of Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa. We have been inspired to do all these things by our spiritual father, His Holiness Jayapatāka Swami. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you always asked us to think big, to try to achieve big things for Kṛṣṇa. Please bless us all to be spiritually fit and sound so we ca accomplish these big things you wanted us to do. For now we are making our personal spiritual lives the biggest priority. Everything else will follow.

You are our spiritual grandfather, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and so we pray that you confer your blessings on our dear spiritual father, His Holiness Jayapatāka Swami, your unflinching servitor, so that he will recover quickly from the serious illness that has beset him. His association and guidance is everything to us now, as he is your perfect representative.

On this auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we beg for your attention and mercy so that we can continue to try to repay you for all that you have done for us by bringing fallen souls to your shelter. Give us the strength to stand up to the many challenges that Māyā puts in front of us constantly so that we may protect ourselves and your institution and its followers.

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda! Thank you for giving us ISKCON, your books, and the wonderful life of Kṛṣṇa consciousness in the association of devotees.

Falling at your divine lotus feet again and again and begging your mercy, we remain  
Your servants in Coimbatore, India.

## Columbus

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my humble obeisances. All glories to you!

Śrīla Prabhupāda, our Columbus center is in the heart of the campus of Ohio State University (OSU). Not many devotees know that this campus is a *tīrtha,* a place of pilgrimage, because of what happened here on May 12, 1969. Here is the account from *Back to Godhead,* published shortly after the event:

Śrīla Prabhupāda arrived Friday, May 9, and our *kīrtana* at the Columbus airport attracted attention in the local papers (“Undoubtedly the most exotic procession ever to grace the airport portals”*Citizen Journal*). Guests crowded the new Hare Kṛṣṇa center to hear a talk delivered as soon as Prabhupāda arrived. . . .

Several days later Prabhupāda and \[Allen\] Ginsberg filled a campus auditorium (which seated 750) to twice the capacity. Students flooded the stage and aisles, and when we started chanting they immediately responded. Within five minutes the chant was out of our control. Over 1,500 students were roaring Hare Kṛṣṇa, standing in their seats, waving their arms, dancing in the aisles and on stage. Ginsberg, sweat pouring down his face, chanted and danced ecstatically. When his garland broke in the melee, we seized the flowers and started throwing them to the students who clambered for them. Then Prabhupāda stood on his dais, and all the students rose to their feet. He gave his garland to a devotee and indicated that we were to throw the flowers to the audience. The students grabbed them and then joined arms to dance. Prabhupāda began jumping up and down and the dais bounced.

All this went on for thirty minutes. Then Ginsberg gave a talk to introduce Prabhupāda, and then Prabhupāda talked. The students were attentive, though hot and exhausted from the chanting and dancing. Then, remarkably enough, we had a second Hare Kṛṣṇa *kīrtana* which was as wild as the first. The sound rose to a grand cataclysmic roar and the stage thundered with dancing. NBC-TV shot film during the whole *kīrtana*. Some students wept with joy. Afterwards Ginsberg said that he had never before seen so many students “burst out their skins” and chant and dance so vigorously and responsively. . . . The following day all the students were talking about it. It seems that Ohio State had never before so completely broken out of its robotic regimen.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, forty years later Columbus has a simple temple on the OSU campus because of the dedicated efforts of your daughters Her Grace Mālatī Prabhu and Her Grace Kāmagiri Dāsī and several of your dedicated granddaughters, especially Her Grace Rasamañjarī Dāsī, who served this temple with her blood and sweat. Today, the temple serves as a spiritual oasis for Ohio State University students, who come to Columbus to get a graduate degree or to realize the great American dream.

I first came to this temple as a casual visitor; then I became hopelessly addicted to *prasādam*. Next I become part of the family, and then I received the cooling shelter of your lotus feet. I spend more time here than at my labs and apartment and never want to leave. The beautiful Deities of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Naṭavara enchant my heart, ŚrīmatīRādhārāṇī’s kitchen is my favorite place to serve, and the bliss I feel when I do some menial service is beyond words. I truly realize the “great American dream” when I read your books and learn what is nescience, what is a dream, and what is reality.

The best part of my life is the Thursday feast at the temple, when we all come together to chant and dance in front of the Deities, associate with other devotees, and honor *prasādam*. I wouldn’t trade it for the opulence of the heavenly planets. In the last few years, countless students from the departments of religious studies, philosophy, and history have witnessed the Hare Kṛṣṇa movement firsthand, many high school students have witnessed your miracle, and many casual visitors have felt their lives irrevocably transformed by the soothing shade of your lotus feet.

By your mercy, our community here has grown steadily so that now we have our Sunday feast in a beautiful church building with exuberant festivals, lively *kīrtanas,* and a well-organized Sunday school. Also, many of your servants and the servants of your servants visit here on their preaching tours. Our temple and festivals also serve as a melting pot for many devotees from nearby places like Athens, Cincinnati, Cleveland, Indianapolis, and Dayton.

Many of your *gṛhastha* grandsons and granddaughters, following your example, are supporting one another, pushing on the movement, preaching, and raising their children in a Kṛṣṇa conscious environment. Seeing the joy on the faces of these children and their enthusiasm to love Kṛṣṇa brings joy and meaning to our lives. The children even put on their first Jagannātha Ratha-yātrā last year, following in your footsteps.

You continue to touch the lives of many here at the university by mercifully inspiring so many activities, such as vegan cooking workshops and *mantra-yoga* programs, where many students get introduced to *kṛṣṇa-prasādam* and the *mahā-mantra.* Many are now chanting the Hare Kṛṣṇa mantra as a daily practice. For the last four years we have been organizing a biannual Transcendence Festival at the university, funded by university grants. At these festivals we meet countless students and distribute your books and *prasadam*.

Jagannātha Dāsa organizes colorful *harināma* festivals in downtown Columbus, goes out on *saṅkīrtana* with books, and is serving the temple very nicely.

We are humbly trying to distribute your books in many ways, like putting your books in all the local motels, libraries, schools, and convenience stores and putting “Smart Boxes” in grocery stores.

By your divine grace there are regular Nāma-haṭṭa programs at the University of Cincinnati, Dayton University, and Ohio University in Athens. Many students are sincerely chanting sixteen rounds and preaching Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

We hope to share Kṛṣṇa consciousness with everyone at the university here and to tell more and more people about the incredible bliss of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. But as we do that, we pray to you for the strength to practice what we preach, and to transform ourselves first. Please give us a speck of dust from your lotus feet, because that alone can deliver every living entity in Columbus. Please give us faith in the holy name, and please let this life be an offering to you. Please break our robotic regimen and make us dance, make us dance as you did in 1969; make us dance as you like in 2009.

Your servants in Columbus, Ohio.  
(written by Bhakta Piyush)

## Dallas

*mūkaṁ karoti vācālaṁ paṅguṁ laṅghayate girim*  
*yat-kṛpā tam ahaṁ vande śrī-guruṁ dīna-tāraṇam*

**I offer my respectful obeisances unto my spiritual master, the deliverer of the fallen souls. His mercy turns the dumb into eloquent speakers and enables the lame to cross mountains.**

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**I offer my respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa on this earth, having taken shelter at His lotus feet.**

**Our respectful obeisances are unto you, O spiritual master, servant of SarasvatīGosvāmī. You are kindly preaching the message of Lord Caitanyadeva and delivering the Western countries, which are filled with impersonalism and voidism.**

*śrī-kṛṣṇa-caitanya prabhu-nityānanda*  
*śrī-advaita gadādhara śrīvāsādi-gaura-bhakta-vṛnda*

*hare kṛṣṇa hare kṛṣṇa kṛṣṇa kṛṣṇa hare hare*  
*hare rāma hare rāma rāma rāma hare hare*

**On Being Found**

You found us.  
We were looking for . . . for what, we didn’t know. And you found us.  
We were everywhere:  
among the skyscrapers in New York,  
in the backwoods of Mississippi,  
in the *Vieux Carre* of New Orleans,  
in Haight-Ashbury of San Francisco,  
in the meadows of Wisconsin,  
in the deserts of Arizona and New Mexico,  
in the forests and mountains of Washington and Oregon.

We were scattered all over North America, making plans:  
to get through the day with something to eat, something to smoke, something to shoot up;  
to experience natural childbirth and breastfeeding;  
to build a place in the country, with a spring and a garden and a wood heater and a clothes line;  
to end wars and discrimination based on race and gender, good looks and money;  
to climb the ladder of corporate America;  
to get a doctorate and teach philosophy or nuclear physics or social studies;  
to be happy.

Many of us were reading Edgar Cayce and Thomas Altizer and Paramhamsa Yogananda and Carlos Casteneda, and trying to discover some way, some mystical way, to relieve the boredom, the pointlessness of our existence. But we didn’t know how.

And then you found us.

Not you, exactly, but your words. Somebody put a book into our hands and told us to read it. Or we came across a book that someone had left in the house we moved into, or that someone had tossed out of the window onto the street, or that someone had forgotten in a public bathroom.

And somehow, we began to read your words. And you were speaking to us. We knew, somehow, that you were answering our questions, that you were answering even questions we hadn’t thought to ask. And that your answers had more substance than anything else we had ever read or heard.

You told us we needed a guide, a spiritual master. And we asked, “Well, then, why don’t *you* be our spiritual master?” But how were we to find you?

For many of us, you sent your disciples, because we were not ready to meet you. But you knew that we needed you; and you told your followers to train us. You just wanted us to be happy and satisfied and respectful and respectable.

You taught us what we needed to know:

how to be clean,  
how to eat the right foods,  
how to avoid doing things that degraded us,  
how to avoid persons who degraded us,  
how to appreciate persons who helped us,  
how to connect with the Supreme Person.

We weren’t even sure there was a Supreme Person. We certainly didn’t know how to get in touch with Him. But you gave us confidence that we could, even within this lifetime. You made the path to Him accessible to us, who could not perform the severe austerities required by other paths.

Because of you, a desire to serve Him awakened in us.

Because of you, millions of books about Him began reaching those who were as hopeless as we once were.

Because of you, temples began springing up all over the planet, giving everyone a chance to meet saintly persons and begin their own spiritual journey.

Because of you, in Dallas, Texas, atheists and agnostics began coming to know the Supreme Person as Kālachandjī, the beautiful moon-faced Lord, and His consort as Rādhārāṇī.

Because of you, in Dallas, Texas, killers of the cow began learning about the glories of cow protection.

Because of you, in Dallas, Texas, persons from all over the planet began coming together as family members, learning to care deeply about each other.

All because of you.

You found us. You loved us. You saved us.

We have no way to repay our debt. Therefore we offer you our lives, our love, our possessions. Please do with us as you will. We wish to become your eternal servants.

We offer our prostrate obeisances to your lotus feet.

Śrīla Prabhupāda-*kī jaya*!

Your servants at Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kālachandjī Dhāma, Dallas, Texas, USA.  
(written by Rūpānuga Dāsa)

## Detroit

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Our dearmost ever well-wisher Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble and respectful obeisances in the dust of your most merciful lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

We are greatly thankful to you for giving us the most merciful Deities Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kuñjabiharījī, Śrī Śrī Jagannātha, Baladeva, and Subhadrā, Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi, and Lord Śrī Nāthajī, along with all your loving and caring devotees. When you bought the Fisher Mansion, you were able to see the future—how this would become the only shelter for so many sincere souls. With your most powerful attractive formula to cure the material disease, many young educated people are accepting Kṛṣṇa consciousness sincerely.

Just yesterday, a group of children as young as twelve, guided by their parents, enjoyed the first-hand experience of staying at the temple and participating in the full temple program and activities of service to Lord Kṛṣṇa. They were so charged up that each of them desired to distribute at least fifteen books of yours in 2009. Their desire can be fulfilled only if you bless them and give them the ability to continue the process for their entire lives. You have empowered Her Grace Premapallavī Dāsī to organize such a transcendental retreat for the youth of today. Every Sunday, Śrīman Ashish Gupta Prabhu is teaching a *Bhagavad-gītā* verse to children before the Sunday Feast, and His Grace Ādi Gopī Prabhu and Neha Mātājī train Sunday school children in Kṛṣṇa conscious activities.

With sincere desire and hard work, Śrīman Lubhit Prabhu and Advaita Prān Prabhu have developed the transcendental presentation through the FATE Museum at the Bhaktivedanta Theater for all the visitors of Devasadan Mandir. You have brought energetic young Śrīmān Jugal Kishore Prabhu to us, who is constantly desiring to have regular *harināma* and book distribution events. Please bless him to fulfill his desires. Vibrant Bhakti Vriksha devotees are eager to participate at the temple under the leadership and systematic training of His Grace Gokul Chandra Prabhu and Śyāma-mañjarīMātājī. Only because of their personal example, Bhakti-Vriksha devotees are always eager to welcome and be most hospitable to all visiting senior Vaiṣṇavas.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, thank you very much for giving us one of your dearest disciples, Her Grace Mālatī Prabhu, as our GBC. Under her loving and caring guidance, we are surviving in these most challenging times. Upgrading of your temple building is going on through the tireless efforts of Śrīman Veṇugopāla Prabhu. The grounds are maintained nicely with the support of His Grace Prabhānu Prabhu and His Grace Pañcajani Prabhu. His Grace Śrī-Nandanandana Prabhu and Bhakta Jim have, through their dedicated vigorous hard work, cultivated the organic vegetable and fruit garden for the Deities. Śrīmān Bharath Prabhu dedicated himself to cultivating an excellent flower garden for the Deities, on top of managing the day-to-day affairs of the temple. Our Ratha-yātrā team is planning for a first-class festival this year. Please bless them so that they can give Lord Jagannātha’s mercy to many residents of Michigan.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have given us everything we need to go back to Godhead, but we are bewildered by the illusory energy. Without your unlimited mercy, we won’t be able to cross the ocean of material existence. We have the tendency to run away from your shelter. Please hold our hands tightly so that we can always remain at your lotus feet in the service of Kṛṣṇa.

Seeking the forgiveness and blessings of you and all your followers, we remain

Your humble and most fallen servants at Devasadan Mandir, Detroit, Michigan, USA.  
(written by Jagad Guru Dāsa)

## East Hartford

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

On January 2, 1973, in a letter to Haṁsadūta Dāsa, you wrote:

Occupational duty means to stick with one type of occupation which is just suitable for me, considering that it is my duty, therefore I am throughout my life obligated to perform it to the best of my ability. This is mature understanding of occupation. That means I must not leave it even for so-called good cause, just like Arjuna wanted to stop his fighting activity just to avoid killing so many of his kinsmen, cousin brothers, and other friends. So we are preachers on behalf of Lord Kṛṣṇa, that is our occupational duty, we haven’t got to search any further some new challenge or change our engagement. No, that has been already settled up. Now best thing will be to develop more and more what we have begun. I have built the skeleton of the building, but there is so much more work remaining before us. . . . You needn’t look further to find some big challenge. The challenge is very near at hand.

You have built the skeleton of the building, and your disciples, granddisciples, and aspiring granddisciples are still working on finishing the inside and outside of this colossal structure.

Here in East Hartford, by the determination of one of your aspiring granddisciples, we have recently started distributing your books by using honor boxes. People take a book and put $2.00 in an attached box. Although we are in the very beginning stages, so far this program has been quite successful. We have also started setting up book tables at different functions to distribute your books, and they have also been doing well. Both of these programs are being carried out by congregational members, and not only are books being distributed, but the congregational members participating in these programs are becoming enlivened.

Our East Hartford temple has been located in the same building for almost thirty years, and by your mercy we have outgrown these facilities. On Sundays, what to speak of holidays, we are getting more people than this temple can adequately support. We are now collecting for a new building, which may cost a million dollars, and so far we have about ten percent of that amount.

You have built the skeleton of this Kṛṣṇa conscious building, and by your mercy and potency the inside and outside are being completed by your disciples, granddisciples, great-granddisciples, and so on. Physically you are no longer present with us, but by your intelligence and desire you have started something that is continuing and will continue to develop exponentially. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for your thoughtful consideration of all the souls who will continue to take birth on this planet for years to come. By your mercy, intelligence, thoughtfulness, and compassion you have given them shelter in your house, the framework of which has been personally built by you. All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Your servants at East Hartford ISKCON, Connecticut, USA.  
(written by Pyāri Mohan Dāsa)

## Eger

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to you on the wonderful day of your appearance!

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, thank you for giving us your endless mercy, through which we have been able to learn the science of devotional service and follow the *bhakti* way. Could any of the roaming souls of the material world be any luckier than we are?

Thank you for engaging us in preaching Kṛṣṇa consciousness. In this way we can participate in your mission and try to begin to repay you for all we have received.

When someone preaches, distributes books, organizes programs, or simply tries to become a devotee or stay a devotee, they can feel how important the proper background for this is. The association of the devotees, the temple, the leadership, the support we get from each other—all these make the above activities possible. Only when we remember that you began everything yourself—alone, without any supporters, in an impersonal, atheistic society in a foreign country—can we realize what great fortitude you had, what unending devotion. Only then can we understand that you possess the purest knowledge, the most perfect surrender, the most generous compassionate heart, and Lord Kṛṣṇa’s authorization.

Every single moment of your life serves as an eternal example for all your followers. It is from you that we have received everything necessary for returning to our eternal home at the end of a life full of devotion. Because of your grace we can live in the shelter of ISKCON and serve you through your devotees. Can we be any luckier than this?

Please bless us to be able to give the process of Kṛṣṇa consciousness to others as well. Please help us extend your mercy to them by leading them to the shelter of your lotus feet. Could they be any luckier than this?

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Your servants in the ISKCON temple in Eger, Hungary.

## Gainesville

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my humble obeisances on this anniversary of your glorious appearance.

Thank you for bringing Kṛṣṇa consciousness to so many. Your gifts of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam, kīrtana,* and *prasādam* continue to bless more and more lives in an increasingly mad world.

When you entered our lives we could not imagine serving Kṛṣṇa would be so serious and yet so fun, so easy and yet so challenging. You knew all this; what’s more, you recognized our endless suffering and its causes. We were oblivious, but you were moved to act.

Even the most jaded person must appreciate your incredible determination. Your legacy is a single, unrelenting effort to set us free.

When you were pushing BTG in Delhi by yourself,  
And tasting all the grit and stench like everybody else,  
Was Vāyu there, with cool, refreshing air for you to breathe?  
Did Indra hold your book-bag so your back would be relieved?  
When people in the tea stalls said “No time! I have to run,”  
Did Sarasvatī grace you with a more persuasive tongue?  
When some untended sacred cow’s horn turned and punctured you,  
Did Dhanvantari bring you herbs and balms till you pulled through?  
Did you find sweat and heatstroke, ridicule and poverty  
A blissful cosmic pastime with supportive deities?  
Or did you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, just push against the odds,  
A simple, frail but focused man, unaided by the gods?  
It’s easy to say “*Śaktyāveśa*—he’s an incarnation.”  
It’s harder to reach out like you, with such determination.  
Religious men can focus, but they sometimes can be smug;  
You knew just how to poke us—but with open-ended love.  
Your sacrificial sweat thus lives as loving ecstasy  
In all your faithful followers who walk in front of me.

Your servants at the ISKCON temple in Gainesville, Florida, USA.  
(written by Kalakaṇṭha Dāsa)

## Gaṅgā Sāgara Baru

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

O most merciful Śrīla Prabhupāda! We would like to thank you for the causeless mercy you have shown us in the form of involving us in the project to create a Vaiṣṇava village on the island of Kalimantan.

We realize that we have no ability to please you, but kindly bestow upon us devotional service that can make us capable of taking part in this project.

Till this day, we have been trying our best to devote ourselves to this project, Gaṅgā Sāgara Baru, but we still feel that we are doing something very insignificant for its development.

On this special occasion, please bestow on us the ability to perform devotional service that will please you. Please bless us to successfully develop this Vaiṣṇava village in a spirit of devotion to our Guru Mahārāja, to Your Divine Grace, and to Śrī Pañca-tattva.

*Jaya* Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Your servants at Gaṅgā Sāgara Baru, Kalimantan Island, Indonesia.  
(written by Sundara Gopāla Dāsa)

## Ghana

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Śrīla Prabhupāda, You Are Very Dear to Our Hearts

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are very dear to our hearts because you bestowed your unlimited mercy upon the world by kindly establishing this movement to give humanity a chance to serve Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are very dear to our hearts because you gave the world your ecstasies and deep realizations in your Bhaktivedanta purports.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are very dear to our hearts because your words carry great potency, the potency of Kṛṣṇa, which turned *mlecchas* like us into devotees.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are very dear to our hearts because you are a great visionary, for you have so designed this movement to serve the spiritual needs of many generations yet unborn.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are very dear to our hearts because you are the best of preachers, for you are expert at saying the right things at the right time.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are very dear to our hearts because you demonstrated your love and care for all by making suitable arrangements to meet the needs of your numerous followers.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are very dear to our hearts because the more we hear your transcendental voice from your lectures, the more we become unafraid of the future and even death.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are very dear to us because your incomparable humility has melted our stonelike sinful hearts and made us fall at your divine lotus feet.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are very dear to our hearts because, as an expert *ācārya,* you perfectly understood the mentalities of everyone—Americans, Europeans, Indians, Chinese, Japanese, Africans, men, women, children, etc.,—and you cut through their cultural preconceptions and conditionings in order to revive their dormant Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are very dear to our hearts because you have given us books that will serve as the law books for humanity for the next ten thousand years.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are very dear to our hearts because you demonstrated great compassion toward all conditioned souls by establishing the BBT to print and distribute your books to the countless fallen souls rotting in this world.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are very dear to our hearts because, while the world slept and snored, you rose early and spoke alone with Kṛṣṇa and then shared those intimate conversations with the whole world in the form of your Bhaktivedanta purports.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you will always remain dear to our hearts because you have given us the process by which we can return to our eternal home, Goloka Vṛndāvana, to be with our eternal lovable Lord, Śrī Kṛṣṇa. Therefore we are forever indebted to you.

Your devotees in Ghana.  
(written by Śrīvāsa Dāsa)

## Gianyar

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,Please accept our most humble obeisances unto your lotus feet.

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are so great, and because of your greatness we now can see that in all countries, cities, towns, and villages people are singing the holy name of Kṛṣṇa. Alone you went to the West to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness and give the people a chance to realize the Absolute Truth. You are saving countless souls from being degraded to a hellish condition of life.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are so kind. You give your causeless mercy to everyone, without considering ethnicity, job, or status. You lift them to a better life and give them a chance to go back home, back to Godhead.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, it is due only to your causeless mercy that we feel we can have a temple here. Although we are working hard to build the temple, it is by your grace that we are endeavoring to have a place where we can experience *sādhu-saṅga* and *kīrtana* and have a center from which to distribute books in our area.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, over the past few years more and more people have come to our temple due to experiencing your mercy in the form of your books. We are always aware that the reason we are expanding here more and more each year is your grace. Without you, we are sure we cannot do anything.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, all of us here are your granddisciples. We feel very lucky that some of your beloved disciples come and give us their association. We have seen your greatness, your grace, and your causeless mercy through your best beloved disciples.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are trying our best to fulfill one of your dreams: to establish a Vaiṣṇava village. We would like to make a *varṇāśrama* village where all devotees can practice *bhakti-yoga* in a peaceful atmosphere. But we are sure that we will not succeed unless we get your causeless mercy.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, by your mercy and by the mercy and efforts of your great disciple His Holiness Bhakti Viśrambha Mādhava Swami, we now have a Tulasī house. We have built this Tulasī house for the pleasure of Kṛṣṇa, Tulasī, and Your Divine Grace. We are very sure that serving Tulasī in this way will enhance our *bhakti*.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for giving us your causeless mercy and allowing us to stay in your big family.

*Jaya* Śrīla Prabhupāda! Śrīla Prabhupāda-*kī jaya*!

Your aspiring servants in Gianyar, Bali, Indonesia.

## Gītā Nāgarī Baru

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

*śrī-caitanya-mano-’bhīṣṭaṁ sthāpitaṁ yena bhū-tale*  
*svayaṁ rūpaḥ kadā mahyaṁ dadāti sva-padāntikam*

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*mūkaṁ karoti vācālaṁ paṅguṁ laṅghayate girim*  
*yat-kṛpā tam ahaṁ vande śrī-guruṁ dīna-tāraṇam*

*śrī-kṛṣṇa-caitanya prabhu-nityānanda*  
*śrī-advaita gadādhara śrīvāsādi-gaura-bhakta-vṛnda*

*hare kṛṣṇa hare kṛṣṇa kṛṣṇa kṛṣṇa hare hare*  
*hare rāma hare rāma rāma rāma hare hare*

My dear, most beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my most humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace, who have so mercifully brought the message of pure devotional service to all fallen souls in this Age of Kali.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are truly Jagat Guru.

Today, on your most glorious appearance day, I would like to submit the following prayer at your lotus feet.

You are fully surrendered to the instructions of your spiritual master, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta SarasvatīGosvāmī Prabhupāda. Because you had full faith in the words of your Guru Mahārāja, you successfully preached Kṛṣṇa consciousness all over the world. Because you came to Indonesia in 1973, now so many devotees have come. This means that total surrender to the words of Guru and Kṛṣṇa guarantees progress in spiritual life and assures success in preaching. So please give mercy to us, please help us became pure in our thinking, speaking, and activities. In this way it will be possible to understand the desires of both Guru and Kṛṣṇa.

You are very kind to us. Although you are not present physically, we feel your presence through your books, your cassette tapes, and your videos, and at times you mercifully appear in the minds, hearts, and dreams of your devotees. We hope to associate with you more and more as time goes on. We remain faithful to your instructions. Please give us the power and intelligence to give up all desires for sense gratification so we may attain real peace, as mentioned in the scriptures:

*vihāya kāmān yaḥ sarvān pumāṁś carati niḥspṛhaḥ*  
*nirmamo nirahaṅkāraḥ sa śāntim adhigacchati*

**“A person who has given up all desires for sense gratification, who lives free from desires, who has given up all sense of proprietorship and is devoid of false ego—he alone can attain real peace.” (*****Bhagavad-gītā*** **2.71)**

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are *patita-pāvana.*

In 1998 there were just a few devotees here at Gītā Nāgarī Baru, but now there are seventeen *gṛhastha* families, for a total devotee population of 104. Our land covers about 60 hectares. This progress is due to your kind mercy, which is manifest through the guidance of our spiritual master, His Holiness Bhakti Rāghava Swami, and His Grace Gaura Maṇḍala Bhūmi Prabhu, and also that of Aṅgalatā-Rādhā Dāsī and other senior devotees. They have all worked very hard to develop the facilities here, which enable us to perform devotional service to Lord Kṛṣṇa and you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. We thank them very much, But we also know that without your blessings and mercy, nothing would be possible.

Of all your transcendental qualities, your compassion and understanding touch us the most. You understand how fallen we are, yet you are compassionate and patient in dealing with us. You are truly *para-duḥkha-duḥkhī*, feeling pain to see the fallen souls suffering. Knowing that only Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s message could deliver us from our ignorance, out of compassion you sacrificed your own comforts and happiness for us to become happies, not hippies.

You are Kṛṣṇa’s bona fide representative in this world, a perfect example of a spiritual master. History will show that your great contribution will be more and more recognized all over the world. Anybody who wants peace, love, and happiness can find these in your books, in your instructions. Your torch of knowledge is guiding people out of ignorance and onto the right path, the way of liberation. Yes, without your books, without your mercy, we could not know who God is, how we can get real happiness, or what we must do to go back to the spiritual world, Kṛṣṇaloka. Thank you for all your gifts, especially the *mahā-mantra.*

Our own realization is lacking, and many times we find ourselves exhausted, frustrated, discouraged, and anxious. This is due entirely to our mundane vision. On this auspicious occasion of your Vyāsa-pūjā, we pray that you kindly give us the necessary strength to continue with enthusiasm and determination in the service of Śrī-Śrī Rādhā-Madana Gopāla, our worshipable Deities. We are convinced that this will please you very much.

Thank you. Hare Kṛṣṇa.

Your servants at Gītā Nāgarī Baru, Tulang Bawang Lampung, Indonesia.  
(written by Ṣaḍbhuja Gaurāṅga Dāsa)

## Gītā-nāgarī

Our most beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to you on this most auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā!

Śrīla Prabhupāda, in 1956 you made this bold statement in an essay entitled “Conception of Gita-Nagari”:

Gita-Nagari shall set the example that neither God nor the living being nor Nature is in any way antagonistic toward one another, but that all of them exist in harmony as a complete unit. When Gita-Nagari will attempt to harmonize such a sweet relation between man and God, man and the world, and the world and God, at such an auspicious time only, the United Nations’ effort to establish peace in the world will be successful, or the dream of a casteless society all over the world will be realized in practice. There is no other practical solution for the scientific basis of universal brotherhood or universal religion than understanding this simple truth of the Fatherhood of Godhead, the Motherhood of Nature, and the Childhood of all living entities.

You had such a vision because the project to become known as Gītā-nāgarī was conceived by you prior to its manifestation! Now decades later, this project still exists, albeit in a simple form. We, the undeserving residents of ŚrīGītā-nāgarī*dhāma,* now fall at your lotus feet, begging you to help us help ourselves. We are begging to develop the sincerity, determination, purity, devotion, love, resilience, and surrender that we know will allow us to transcend our external and internal constraints. When we do this, we open ourselves up to becoming your instruments. Please let us be excited by this proposition, and not hesitant or fearful. Please grant us the strength to move past the obstacles that have gathered and begin afresh, with renewed vigor, spiritual perspective, commitment, co-operation, and love—love for you, for our respective *dīkṣā* gurus, for Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Dāmodara, and for each other.

Beneath the external simplicity of this farm lies the *dhāma,* with our ever-merciful and beautiful Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Dāmodara, placid cows, rich and fertile land for agriculture, and all the associated pastimes. How fortunate we are to have the greatest opportunity to engage every day, every moment, in service to the Supreme Lord. Only you can offer us this—you who have revealed to us materially conditioned souls the topmost activities, personalities, and residences.

For all that you have unconditionally offered us—your compassion, mercy, love, sacrifice, tolerance, faith—we can show our gratitude only through our surrender and our commitment to fulfilling your desires for ŚrīGītā-nāgarī*dhāma*. We therefore beseech you to kindly consider our prayer that you inspire us to take seriously and to heart your instructions and desires. As this planet rapidly sinks into deeper and deeper levels of material distress, let us contribute to the upliftment of the human condition by offering ŚrīGītā-nāgarī*dhāma* as the spiritual sanctuary for all who seek shelter. With your blessings on this all-auspicious day, let us begin the journey to sustainability, simple living and high thinking, self-sufficiency, and a glorious spiritual culture for each other and the generations of your devoted servants to come.

Thank you, our most beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Your grateful and eager servants at Śrī Gītā-nāgarī dhāma, Port Royal, Pennsylvania, USA.

## Hari Nāma Deśa

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet. On this most auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā, we offer you this humble attempt to glorify you in our limited ways.

We wish we had numberless tongues to glorify you. Listening to the memories of your disciples and contemporary witnesses, we find it impossible to grasp your achievements, your glory, your mercy, your determination, your devotion, and all the other transcendental qualities of your divine personality.

Thank you for appearing on this planet. Without you we would have never heard about Lord Kṛṣṇa. Thank you for your teachings. We offer you our best wishes for your happiness, because we believe that you are near to Lord Kṛṣṇa.

It is because of your inconceivable mercy and tireless efforts that many millions of people around the globe have come to know about Kṛṣṇa and the genuine meaning of life, and so have begun practicing spiritual life seriously. You have given us Kṛṣṇa in the form of the holy name, many books, and pure devotees, without whose guidance we could not find the way out of darkness and ignorance.

As a fisher of fallen souls, you have put out the bait of love and with an unexpected sudden yank pulled us out of the ocean of material existence! Blinded by the bright light of your glory and the effulgence of your stalwart disciples, who have been serving you as leaders of your society for the past thirty years or more, we are wriggling, flapping, and gasping at the end of your line, like ridiculous little fish out of water.

We are so glad to always have you in our hearts, guiding us. We are thankful for your love and your patience with us. Please always escort us on our way.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are so magnanimous, loving, knowledgeable, and universally capable that we are compelled, despite ourselves, to surrender daily, hourly, and every second to the mercy of your lotus feet.
