As a great servant of Lord Kṛṣṇa, you have demonstrated by your life’s example everything that you have preached, and thus you have attracted and fascinated thousands of souls.

Will we ever be able to understand what you did for us? Please allow us to always remember this inconceivable gift, so that we may be able to pass on this spark of good fortune.

Out of your deep compassion you have taken us out of our deep darkness and shown us the way. We will never be able to honorably offer enough thanks for your inconceivable gift.

Thanks to your great determination, we and thousands of other people, even decades after your departure, are still getting an opportunity to understand the true goal of life and revive our relationship with Kṛṣṇa. For that we are eternally indebted to you.

You have left your ISKCON with the task of saving the world from Māyās onslaught. You have given us the framework. While trying to understand your desire and your method of teaching, we find that in the process of rigorous confrontation with controversial issues we get, by your mercy, a more mature understanding of your instructions.

Please escort us on our long way to Kṛṣṇa. Your books are our guidance. They help us try to act in virtue and help us in the struggle against our ignorance.

With the books you wrote and the devotees you made, you gave us a part of that internal peace that all souls are searching for. But because of ignorance we are searching in the wrong places. Dear Prabhupāda, savior of the most fallen, let us be only a small part of your eternal service.

Trapped as we are in this material body, our life is of short duration. Therefore please continue to carry us in your lotus hands, sheltered, up to the end of our lives, showing us the right way back home to Godhead.

Please strengthen our desire and our determination to serve you. Please help us overcome the complacency and laziness that paralyze us. Please remove all distractions that prevent us from being focused exclusively on your service. Please forgive our sins and cleanse away the *anarthas* that weaken us. Please help us always think of you with deep gratitude for your causeless love and grace.

Those who have the service of Lord Kṛṣṇa and His scriptures as an ally, dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, will also be engaged until their last breath in the Lord’s devotional service. This you have proven yourself.

We thank all the devotees, and especially you, dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, for your grace and the mercy that you gave us and will hopefully always continue to give us.

We are all so glad to have had the opportunity to know you and your devotees, Śrīla Prabhupāda. During difficult times, we are all so much in need of warmth and cordiality. In your movement we do find persons to address our questions to and we do get answers from them, answers that help us make our own decisions in life.

When we hear the devotees’ wonderful glorifications of you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we become very much touched and filled with feelings of joy. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you always fill us with peace and a disposition to share love with you and others. Our hearts palpitate because the feeling is so pleasant to our souls.

We just want to thank you so much for the creation of your beautiful ISKCON, which is as beautiful as Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa himself.

Over the short span of twelve years, Your Divine Grace flooded the entire world with Kṛṣṇa consciousness, thus fulfilling the prediction made by Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

You are love personified. We are touched when we read your books. We feel touched when we close our eyes.

Please accept our very humble obeisances and let us thank you for your never-ending mercy.

From the heart, we thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Your humble devotees in Hari Nāma Deśa, ISKCON Wiesbaden, Germany.

## Jagannātha Mandira

Dear respected Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my humble obeisances. All glories to you!

We are very fortunate that your disciples are running this movement very expertly. When you were in Kolkata, you told me about your birthplace. So accordingly I gave my house to ISKCON Kolkata. We are planning to make a nice museum and temple in the house of your birthplace. I know it is very difficult work to establish a proper preaching center there, but I hope that if you bless me and guide me, everything can be possible. If I could fulfill your desire as one of your family members, my whole life will be successful.

I visited your Kṛṣṇa-Balarāma temple and met your disciple Gaḍi Dāsa. He gave me a nice place to stay at the MVT guesthouse. I am very happy to see the advancement of the devotees at your temple. Your beloved son Gopāla Kṛṣṇa Goswami is running the Kṛṣṇa-Balarāma temple, as well as the RādhāPārtha-sārathi temple in Delhi, so nicely. It indicates that your spiritual potency is working through the devotees. Though you are not physically here with us, we see that in Māyāpur, Vṛndāvana, Delhi, and Mumbai, all your favorite places, your followers are preaching Kṛṣṇa consciousness vigorously. The whole world will come to visit these four places to adopt this Kṛṣṇa conscious philosophy. You are always with us in your books and instructions and with your beloved disciples.

Please forgive me if I have made any offense unto your lotus feet. Please bless me, as you are continuously doing, so that I can give more service to your temples. This is my prayer unto your lotus feet.

Your servants at the Jagannātha Mandira in Godrumadvīpa, Navadvīpa, West Bengal, India.  
(written by Saṅkarṣaṇa Dāsa, Śrīla Prabhupāda’s nephew)

## Japan

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Surrounded on all sides by the sea, before the advent of air travel Japan had no other way to acquire knowledge and culture except by ships from abroad. And so Buddhists underwent tremendous hardships to bring their scriptures in from China; their ships were frequently wrecked by violent storms, and both monks and scriptures sank to the bottom of the ocean.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, your voyage was much harder than theirs. You were aged and had to endure a long voyage from Calcutta to New York, crossing the Atlantic Ocean on the cargo ship *Jaladuta*. And none of your godbrothers helped you: you embarked alone. Though raging storms and heart attacks threatened your life, with immovable determination and faith in the words of your spiritual master, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura, you surpassed all the difficulties. All glories to you!

Now we, your disciples and followers all over the world, are feeling greatly indebted to you for your efforts, which gave us real life. To repay our obligation, we are trying to assimilate and follow your instructions, to become lovers of Kṛṣṇa.

As a representative of Kṛṣṇa, even now you are delivering so many conditioned souls via your transcendental books. We have full faith in your words “Books are basis.”

Unfortunately, we have not published your books in Japanese for such a long time, and we are so sorry and ashamed about it. Please forgive us for our laziness and dullness. But now we have news that will definitely make you smile.

The power and ability of one man is limited, but, as a proverb goes, two heads are better than one, and the result can be multiplied when we all put our heads together with enthusiasm. In this spirit, we have just begun the “Prabhupāda Translation Project,” through which we plan to translate *all* your books. *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* is now being translated.

Not many people here in Japan know you yet. To repay you for your causeless mercy on us, we are endeavoring to join together firmly, become one instrument, and translate your words into Japanese.

We are all originally Kṛṣṇa’s servants and fully conscious of Him. But how difficult it is to realize this simple truth! We are now on the great voyage to reach this final destination. Far ahead of our ship, we see the land of Kṛṣṇa, appearing and disappearing. Under the bottom of the ship spread the unfathomable depths of the ocean. Surrounded by various kinds of dangers on all sides, we have no other shelter but to tightly grasp your lotus feet. As the captain of the ship, please guide us and save us so that we will be able to follow your footsteps—*yasya prasādād bhagavat-prasādaḥ*

Your servants in Japan.

## Kaduna

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

We offer our humble obeisances unto you.

Once again your Vyāsa-pūjā celebration is affording us the opportunity to review the quality of our obeisances unto your divine feet. Are we truly following your basic instructions—observing the four regulative principles, chanting our prescribed number of rounds of the *mahā-mantra,* and regularly studying your books to understand the rudiments of devotional service? Are we establishing and managing our temples so well that the devotees become attached to the service of guru and Kṛṣṇa, thereby inspiring the congregation to feel an obligation to the temple? Are we establishing self-sufficient farm communities that allow every devotee to gradually progress on the path of *bhakti*? Are we exemplifying the teachings of Your Divine Grace in such a way that the larger society looks up to us for solutions to their socio-political and spiritual problems?

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we humbly request you to arrange to bring back your Society to your original vision, where every devotee finds a space to joyfully participate in making the Society successful.

The devotees in the ISKCON temple in Kaduna, Nigeria.  
(written by Cāṇakya Paṇḍita Dāsa)

## Kaundinyapur

We, your granddisciples at ISKCON Kaundinyapur, offer our most humble obeisances unto your lotus feet on your most auspicious Vyāsa-pūjā day.

We feel ourselves most fortunate to be able to enjoy Kṛṣṇa consciousness due to your mercy.

Here in Śrīdhām Kaundinyapur, the place where Śrī Rukmiṇī Devī appeared, ISKCON now has five acres of land. Under the blissful guidance of your empowered *sannyāsī* disciple Lokanāth Swami, a wonderful center has been established, with a temple, *gurukula, gośālā,* and guesthouse, and now gardens are taking shape, along with *sannyāsī* and *bhakta āśrama*s. We will then be able to better distribute your books and run all our preaching activities, thus fully spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness in the whole area.

On this all-auspicious Vyāsa-pūjā day, we dedicate ourselves to serving you with renewed vigor and all humility.

The devotees of ISKCON Kaundinyapur, India.  
(written by Akrūra Dāsa)

## Kṛṣṇa Balarāma Kṣetra

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

All the devotees at ISKCON Kṛṣṇa-Balarāma Kṣetra pay their respectful obeisances at your lotus feet.

We have a sprawling fourteen-acre farm housing a joint family and few *brahmacārīs*. The farm is located near Udupi, also known as Dakshina Vrindavan, the seat of Śrīla Madhvācārya. The predominating Deities at Kṛṣṇa Balarāma Kṣetra are Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Kṛṣṇa-Balarāma and Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi. The community includes ten cows and a pair of working bulls. The bulls are fully engaged in agriculture and transportation.

We humbly want to offer at your lotus feet the report of the first session of the *varṇāśrama* college. Almost thirty years ago you ordered, “Immediately establish the *varṇāśrama* college.” Although there were some attempts made, this order was largely unfulfilled. Under the guidance of His Holiness Bhakti Rāghava Mahārāja, we have successfully completed the training of the first batch of students in the *varṇāśrama* college. The course was entitled “Land, Cows, and Kṛṣṇa.” The students were trained in agriculture, cow protection, traditional mud construction, traditional village technologies, traditional arts and crafts, Āyurveda, and *yoga āsanas*. Various teachers were invited to teach parts of this course. We hope to continue to offer this course and others every year, and we seek your blessings to be able to do so.

By your mercy alone we are able to understand the importance of simple living and high thinking, and we ourselves are experiencing the benefits of this Krsna conscious life away from the passion and ignorance that dominate the cities.

Always indebted to you, we remain  
Your lowly servants at Kṛṣṇa-Balarāma Kṣetra, Hebri, Udupi, India.

## Kṛṣṇa Balarāma Kṣetra II

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our respectful obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace! All glories to Śrī Guru and Gaurāṅga!

“ISKCON is a preaching movement,” you said, while at the same time you emphasized, “Now is time to boil the milk.”

At Śrī Jagannātha Mandir, we are trying to do just that. In order to adhere to your instructions, we have a two-pronged approach: we are attempting to focus on spiritual internalization while at the same time trying to spread the mission of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

Over the last four or five years, we have gradually amended the imbalances that were present in our *yātrā,* where external or preaching and expansion concerns had outweighed the spiritual content. Primarily through the Spiritual Counselor System, we have gradually trained and guided our devotees in the essence of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, especially in the core areas of *sādhana* and *sad-ācāra*.

We have seen how, over this period, a positive evolution has occurred among many devotees in our congregation. We have seen more solidarity, seriousness in *sādhana,* general improvement in Vaiṣṇava qualities and dealings, and greater dedication to *seva*.

New devotees are quickly taking up *bhakti-yoga* seriously and devotedly. The senior devotees are becoming more involved in temple services and being steadfast in *sādhana*. How could all this happen if not for your magnanimity? We are nothing but instruments in your hands.

On the preaching front we are adding various innovative strategies, inspired by Your Divine Grace. The *bhakti* homes, college-level preaching, and Bengali preaching are additions since last year.

The Dāmodara Preaching Program is reaching newer heights annually, with the participation of almost the entire Malaysian *yātrā* last year. This resulted in an astounding figure of 80,000 people offering ghee lamps in one month.

Last year the Congregational Development Minister gave us the honor of leading a global expansion of the Dāmodara program. A four-man team from Kuala Lumpur heeded the call and visited a few highly promising nations, where they introduced this innovative preaching tool.

Your favorite *seva,* book distribution, is also growing gradually, while Food for Life *seva* has steadily maintained its meal distribution program. Our FFL team has some ambitious plans to expand in the near future. *Harināma saṅkīrtana* has also been increasing in frequency steadily, to about four hundred *nāgar saṅkīrtanas* in 2008.

By your mercy, the internal operations of Deity *seva,* festivals, and temple operations are being managed satisfactorily.

While trying hard to develop preaching in Kuala Lumpur and Selangor state, we are also trying to assist other *yātrās* in Malaysia through guidance in the area of community development and preaching.

Though the potential for preaching in Malaysia is immense, we are falling short due to our own shortcomings and lack of purity. We pray for your continued mercy and benedictions so we may develop more zeal and enthusiasm to carry on with the mission of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

On this auspicious occasion of your Vyāsa-pūjā, we beg to remain at the shelter of your lotus feet and serve you eternally. If you are kind to us, naturally Kṛṣṇa will be kind to us. We humbly offer whatever little successes we have achieved at your divine lotus feet.

Please forgive us for all our offenses and keep guiding us throughout our lives, for you are the expert navigator of the ship known as ISKCON.

Your humble spiritual grandchildren and servants at ISKCON’s Śrī Jagannātha Mandir, Kuala Lumpur, Malaysia.

## Lagos

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to you!

On this auspicious day of your appearance, we, your grandchildren, have come to pay homage unto Your Divine Grace.

Today we are full of joy and happiness because we are the proud beneficiaries of a grandfather who is known all over the three worlds for his unlimited compassion, mercy, and selflessness.

By dint of that compassion, we are sheltered from the onslaught of the material world in general and Kali-yuga in particular. Under the roof of the “house you built in which the whole world can live,” we feel secure and protected, provided we follow your divine instructions and standards.

Even though you are not physically present, we feel your presence through your disciples, who are our spiritual fathers. They are the kind extension of your compassion to guide us on the path of *bhakti*. We thank you for this wonderful gift, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Even though you are not physically here, we fell your heavy, living presence on every page of the many volumes of books you painstakingly produced to serve as the standard law books for generations to come. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for this invaluable gift to the world.

Even though you are not physically present, we feel your presence in the Food for Life program, as well as in the Govinda’s restaurants and farm settlements you kindly established just to help us get connected to Lord Kṛṣṇa. In fact, your glories are unlimited. Without you, what would be our lot? We are therefore very grateful to you for making all these arrangements to save us.

Yet, we still have one more request to make. The father is generally kind and loving to his children, but the grandfather is even more kind, loving, and caring. We therefore crave your indulgence: Kindly forgive and tolerate our frailties and frivolities. We are insignificant members of a subbranch of your big ISKCON tree. Please bless and empower us to extend your compassion and mercy to others.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, may you live forever through your instructions.

Śrīla Prabhupāda-*kī jaya*!

Your struggling granddisciples in Lagos, Nigeria.  
(written by Guru Dāsa)

## Lalitā-pura Seva-kuñja

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

All glories to Śrī Guru and Gaurāṅga! All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we, your servants who perform devotional service in Lalitā-pura Seva-kuñja *āśrama,* would like to offer our respectful obeisances unto your lotus feet. May all of us be able to carry out your orders and spread your transcendental message, which describes the actual path to real bliss.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we always pray to you that this Lalitā-pura Seva-kuñja *āśrama* may one day become a useful place for everyone who desires to serve Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa. Also, by your mercy may this *āśrama* implement the system of *varṇāśrama-dharma,* a system that can help us constantly remember Kṛṣṇa.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, by implementing *varṇāśrama-dharma* here, we will be able to always think of Kṛṣṇa and become free from the miseries of this material world. And if we do not implement this *varṇāśrama* system in the Kṛṣṇa consciousness society, there won’t be any success in the social order. Therefore, we would like to thank Your Divine Grace, who have given us the path that will actually bring us back home, back to Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, there are no words we can glorify you with that will please you more than our just following your instructions. May we one day be able to promote this *āśrama* as a place that is bringing you pleasure by helping to fulfill your desire to implement *varṇāśrama-dharma*.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, may we always be able to sing your glories here in Lalitā-pura Seva-kuñja *āśrama*.

Your servants at Lalitā-pura Seva-kuñja āśrama, Palangka Raya, Indonesia.  
(written by Brajarāja Dāsa)

## Las Vegas

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda, my beloved spiritual grandpa,  
You are the greatest Vaiṣṇava and genuine messenger of the *paramparā*.  
You are my Lord and my transcendental grandmaster;  
Only your divine mercy makes my spiritual progress go faster.  
You are a genius and a great Vaiṣṇava of topmost qualification,  
Founder-*ācārya* of the ISKCON worldwide transcendental nation.  
Your perfect Kṛṣṇa consciousness presentation  
Gives human society the highest liberation.  
You preached Lord Kṛṣṇa’s pure message to human society  
To relieve everybody from suffering and constant anxiety.  
Your determined teachings were full of loving compassion;  
Those who listened to you were purified from degrading ignorance and passion.  
Although modern human society is full of material bodily designations,  
You preach to everybody without discriminations.  
Your merciful, full-of-tolerance-and-compassion preaching  
Is giving enlightenment to the heart of every human being.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the world’s foremost master of *bhakti;*  
By converting Kali-yuga people into *kṛṣṇa-bhaktas* you manifest your divine *śakti*.  
You have built a beautiful ISKCON, the transcendental abode,  
Where various people from different nations can live under the same code.  
You and your earthly success were predicted by your god–great-grandpa Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura,  
Who saw long ago with his transcendental vision your project at Śrī Dhām Māyāpur.  
You came to Westerners to remind them of what they had forgot,  
To help them revive their eternal relationship with Kṛṣṇa and awaken their dormant love of God.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda, you were like a loving father, the best well-wisher;  
You introduced the traditional dress codes, hygiene, etiquette feature,  
Along with Vedic cuisine, weddings, festivals, music, drama, and dance.  
You did this all alone because you are the best teacher of highest competence.  
You taught us how to perform loving devotional service to the Lord,  
To get Kṛṣṇa’s merciful divine support and spread love of Godhead all over the world.  
You are the purest of the pure, the greatest earthly prophet of all,  
*Mahā-patita-pāvana,* savior of suffering conditioned souls.  
One who reads your books is no longer morose  
And will achieve the highest transcendental goals.  
You astounded the literary and academic communities  
By translating and writing commentary on Vedic wisdom immensities.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda, your books are highly respected by the academic authority  
For their prestige, depth, knowledge, and clarity.  
You published over seventy volumes of books on the classical Vedic civilization,  
Constituting a comprehensive library of Vedic philosophy, religion, and cultural presentation.  
Your writings have been translated into over fifty languages  
To help people understand Kṛṣṇa’s true transcendental images.  
Complete sets of your books have been purchased by the libraries,  
As well as by many other institutional visionaries.  
Even professors of major universities around the world,  
Including Princeton, Harvard, Yale, Oxford, Cambridge, and Sorbonne,  
Use your books as standard texts in college courses students take on.  
You patiently tried to educate everybody  
That we are not this material mind and body  
But are eternal, full-of-knowledge and blissful spirit soul  
Who, when spiritually awakened, will always chant Nitāi-Gaura Haribol!  
Śrīla Prabhupāda, you stressed *yuga-dharma* education  
And propagated the chanting of the Lord’s holy names with great dedication.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda, in twelve years you spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness all over the world  
As nobody in human history had spread it before.  
During that time you established more than a hundred spiritual centers,  
Rural communities, schools, publishing firms, institutes, and temples.  
You circled the globe fourteen times on lecture tours on six continents,  
And still found time to write prolifically and teach with insight immense.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda your compassionate heart  
Has no equal in this universal part.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda you are extremely dear to Kṛṣṇa. He empowered you to such an extent that you were able to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness throughout the entire world. Śrīla Prabhupāda, a few days before you left this planet, your godbrother Purī Mahārāja said, “You have saved millions of people around the world. You should be called *mahā-patita-pāvana,* the great savior of the fallen conditioned souls.”

Śrīla Prabhupāda, I beg you to make me free from lust, desire, and anger like fire,  
So I can spiritually elevate in Kṛṣṇa consciousness higher and higher.  
Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, please protect my lusty heart from degradation  
And help me to attain the highest spiritual purification.  
Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, please help me follow the *bhakti-yoga* process  
As you want me to, and stop me from being a number-one nonsense.  
Thus engage me in your preaching mission so intense.  
Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, please empower me to make my offering to you very lovely  
In the form of bringing to your lotus feet at least one conditioned soul monthly.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda, you’ve made me preach in Las Vegas, where challenges are huge,  
So I beg you to help me accept your lotus feet as my only refuge.  
Please bless me with the shower of your divine mercy, which I badly need.  
I beg you to let me stay forever in the dust of your lotus feet,  
Because this is the only way to get rid of every material weed  
And cultivate within my heart the *bhakti-latā* seed.

Your servants in Las Vegas, Nevada, USA.  
(written by Surapāla Dāsa)

## Leon

Dearmost Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most respectful obeisances at the dust of your magnanimous lotus feet.

Every year, on your appearance day, it is a great pleasure to remember your most sacred person and activities, and of course your great sacrifice in coming to save us.

Every grateful Vaiṣṇava in the Western countries should be aware of your incredibly magnanimous boldness in taking all risks so that we could be able to taste the flavor of the holy name and the pleasure of serving the Supreme Lord, Kṛṣṇa. You widely opened the bridge toward Kṛṣṇa-loka.

We will never be able to repay even a tiny fraction of our debt to you for what you did and are doing for us, Śrīla Prabhupāda. We will always be indebted to you. Of course, it is a great pleasure to be indebted to you, because it means that as long as we remember our great and unrepayable debt to you, we will be connected to the most ecstatic *seva:* trying to attract the conditioned souls to the service of your lotus feet.

We deeply thank you for everything you’ve done and are doing for us, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Please, please allow us to always remain your most indebted but grateful servants.

Your servants in Leon, Guanajuato, Mexico.

## Lome

**In Spite of What We Are**

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my prostrated obeisances. All glories to you!

This particular year I have a special realization in my service to Guru and Kṛṣṇa. My Guru Mahārāja, His Holiness Bhakti Tīrtha Swami, of the blessed memory, often said to us in Africa that in spite of what we are (and of course that meant a lot, considering our various backgrounds full of passion and ignorance), your mission, Śrīla Prabhupāda, would continue to make the necessary impact.

When you were physically present, Śrīla Prabhupāda, devotees were certain that Kṛṣṇa consciousness was going to bring about a new world order. Because of your holy presence and your holy association, devotees all over the world believed in your vision and were convinced that Kṛṣṇa consciousness was the remedy for the world’s problems.

But when you removed your *vapu* association and the meaning of *ācārya* became confounded, we were all asking “What happened to Prabhupāda’s vision?” The promised land became a utopia and all the ideals of a Kṛṣṇa conscious world under Kṛṣṇa conscious leaders become like a reverie. But in spite of the shortcomings or inaccurate representations that begin to cloud the vision, many wonderful historic landmarks are dotting the map of the world of the Hare Kṛṣṇas. So in spite of us, it appears that the program of expanding the frontiers of Kṛṣṇa consciousness cannot be checked by man’s imperfections.

Under the able leadership of your illustrious son Bhakti Tīrtha Swami, we firmly believed in your axiom that the word “impossible” is to be found in a fool’s dictionary. With him in front, we knew we would win many marathons together. Book distribution would not be just a lip service, but a loving service, Opening of new centers and construction of temples would not be wasted efforts but important steps in the effort to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Because he was always there for us, we reposed all our trust in him.

In his physical absence, though, the promised land is becoming further remote. Everyone who claimed they knew him the best are doing things he would never have authorized. Temples sold, lands abandoned, devotees in disarray, different philosophies for different occasions. You taught the highest philosophy for all times, and its basic points have always been well known and consistent:

Preaching is the essence.  
Books are the basis.  
Utility is the principle.  
Purity is the force.

Do the needful.  
First deserve, then desire.  
Work now, samādhi later.  
Love and trust.  
Ācāra-pracāra.

A Vaiṣṇava is a perfect gentlemen.  
First dress, then address.  
Etc.

The principles you taught may appear like common sense, but common sense is not at all common. “A bird in the hand is worth two in the bush” is a simple English adage, but that kind of simple common sense does not prevail when we are ready to give up certainty for uncertainty.

Yet in spite of us and all our numerous mistakes, Kṛṣṇa is still arranging many ways in which His glories may be known. Recently in Ghana there was an official state funeral for the foster father of ex-President Rawlings. Mr. Rawlings had written in his will that he should be cremated and that the Hare Kṛṣṇas should officiate at his funeral. He particularly stated that he did not want a Christian pastor at his funeral. It was indeed an historic event. After the devotee’s sermon, all the ministers and other government dignitaries, including the former President, were demanding Kṛṣṇa conscious literatures. This was beyond any preaching engagement we could have organized on our own. This is an example of how you bestow your mercy on devotees who are sincerely trying to carry out your order.

So in spite of us, and despite what we are, another property will soon be acquired in Togo as a permanent residence for Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā–Govinda-Mādhava. Whether or not ISKCON as a society offers assistance for acquiring property for preaching, the Indian community here has already advanced $40,000 as partial payment for acquiring this property. Though we are still appealing to kind-hearted, generous donors for financial aid to acquire the property, we are convinced that in spite of us, Kṛṣṇa has his own arrangement for accomplishing His divine mission, and we can only be instruments.

So I have been meditating seriously on my Guru Mahārāja’s words, and I see that he was perfectly correct. In spite of us, Śrīla Prabhupāda, your mission will go from strength to strength. And now more than ever, I strongly believe in the *Gītās* statement that “Wherever there is Kṛṣṇa, the master of all mystics, and wherever there is Arjuna, the supreme archer, there will also certainly be opulence, victory, extraordinary power, and morality.”

On this day of your appearance, Śrīla Prabhupāda, I fervently pray that you give us the vision to be able to see Kṛṣṇa everywhere, and that you make us into high-caliber devotees who can make His glory known to the world.

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Your servants in Lome, Togo (West Africa).  
(written by Varāha Dāsa)

## London (Soho)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to you! All glories to Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Londonīśvara.

We feel most fortunate to be serving at ISKCON’s London center this year, as 2009 marks the fortieth anniversary of the start of ISKCON’s preaching in London and the thirtieth anniversary of the founding of Govinda’s restaurant.

How grateful we are to you, dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, for fanning the spark of devotion in the hearts of the devotees who took so many risks to come to London and establish the Bury Place Temple.

How we delight in the pastime of how you met your beloved Deities Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Londonīśvara. While learned yogīs take many lifetimes to capture the Lord in the heart, you took just minutes! And forty years on we have been given the priceless opportunity of performing some insignificant service for Them.

How many fortunate souls have traversed the steps of our temple?

How many devotees have occupied the post of temple president, temple commander, head *pūjārī, saṅkīrtana* leader, and so forth?

So many spirit souls have come and gone, and now we stand on the eve of the fortieth anniversary celebrations, feeling both humbled and grateful to have also received your mercy and compassion under the umbrella of ISKCON London.

We pray that you always remain prominent in our lives through your instructions.

We also pray that we may somehow imbibe the urgency you always preached about to become Kṛṣṇa conscious *now*.

We remain your surrendered servants and hope that we can somehow please you and your beloved Deities Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Londonīśvara during this special year marking the most special moment when you came to save the conditioned souls of London.

Your aspiring servants at ISKCON London (Soho), UK.  
(written by Bhāva Bhakti Devī Dāsī)

## London (south)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept the humble obeisances of all your granddisciples from South London at the dust of your divine lotus feet on this, the 113thanniversary of your appearance on this earth planet.

**S**ervice to all of mankind by going round the world and opening up temples.  
**R**eeling in the hippies and making them happies by giving them Kṛṣṇa *prasādam.*  
**I**nspiring all and sundry by the vast library of books you left us.  
**L**ovingly spreading the holy name through the *saṅkīrtana* parties of your disciples  
**A**nd granddisciples, who want to help you fulfill your desires to please your spiritual master.  
**P**lease keep on sending us your disciples to guide us and remind us of who we are,  
**R**epresenting you and ultimately Kṛṣṇa,  
**A**lways tirelessly fighting Māyā to protect your growing family,  
**B**ringing your books alive by your guidance,  
**H**oping to save all the souls still rotting in this material pool,  
**U**ltimately sending the poor souls to you in the spiritual world,  
**P**roving that you are still with us and caring for what happens to us,  
**A**nd fulfilling your promise that you love us with all your heart  
**D**espite all our faults and crazy thinking,  
**A**nd above all elevating us to the human platform through your books.

We thank you for being so kind and providing us with a guide map to come out of this material world via your books and disciples, and we pray that we can serve you in whatever way we can to help you spread the holy names.

Your fallen granddisciples in South London, UK.

## Longdenville

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept out humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace on this most auspicious anniversary of your appearance in this world!

Śrīla Prabhupāda, for a conditioned soul to be able to glorify you is impossible, but, taking shelter of your devotees, we make this humble attempt.

When Kṛṣṇa came five thousand years ago, He established *dharma* in the form of the *Bhagavad-gītā*. By doing so He gave such a sublime and expansive vault of knowledge that the *Gītā* has touched not only the lives of the Vaiṣṇavas, but countless others as well. In the same way, your coming to the West brought with it a renaissance of human thought through the medium of your transcendental books.

Over the last two years we have offered weekly classes at the temple to systematically study your books, and thus far we have gone through the *Bhagavad-gītā* and *The Nectar of Instruction*. We have also begun a twice-monthly study of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* and are currently studying *The Nectar of Devotion*. The idea of these classes is not to make scholars of the devotees or to mechanically study your books, but rather to help the devotees appreciate these wonderful gems of your life’s work.

While studying your books, I found the most amazing thing. More and more I realized that every word was not just written but rather carefully placed. Just as when a person assembles a puzzle from its various pieces each piece has only one correct position, so when you penned your purports from the infinite sky of your realizations your precisely placed each word to indicate a specific meaning and intention.

Another amazing thing we found was how you have so carefully interwoven the deepest conclusions of the *ācāryas* amidst seemingly “basic purports.” An example of this is found in the *Bhagavad-gītā:*

\[So\] a Kṛṣṇa conscious man maintains himself by his work just to remain fit for action in the transcendental loving service of the Lord. He is therefore immune to all the reactions of his endeavors. Like an animal, he has no proprietorship even over his own body. A cruel proprietor of an animal sometimes kills the animal in his possession, yet the animal does not protest. Nor does it have any real independence. \[*Bhagavad-gītā* 4.21, purport\]

In this passage the man in full knowledge is being described. When reading it we were amazed. A casual reader will find that it deals with the self-realized soul, but one who reads deeper may discover a very esoteric sentiment, that of the love of the *gopīs* for Kṛṣṇa. And who has a greater realization than those most beloved of Kṛṣṇa?

These are just two examples of the amazing gift you have given us and the world.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, on this momentous occasion, we simply pray that we may be able to serve you by gaining more appreciation for you books, not only by distributing them for others’ benefit but by “distributing” them to ourselves. We pray that day by day we may be able to serve your great mission with more and more enthusiasm and give greater care to those who are showing interest—even the faintest interest—in the path of *kṛṣṇa-bhakti*.

Your humble servants at the Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gopīnātha Mandir, Longdenville, Trinidad

## Malaga

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

Every day in our temple we follow the spiritual program you established for all of us: waking up early for *maṅgala-ārati*, chanting sixteen rounds of *japa,* greeting Their Lordships (Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi) and holding *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* class—all in the good association of devotees. By your causeless mercy, the mere application of your instructions through this daily program—whether with devotion or at least out of obedience—is softening our hearts, and little by little we are understanding more and more what spiritual life is all about and who Śrī Kṛṣṇa is.

As we are experiencing in our personal lives that your vision and precepts are completely perfect and bestow tremendous spiritual benefit, we are similarly confident that your proposition of a way of life based on simple living and high thinking is perfect in all respects and will benefit not only our ISKCON Society but also society at large.

You envisioned the devotees living together in rural communities, in the open space of the countryside; you envisioned our economy being based on the land and the cows and their products, and you envisioned a society organized on the basis of people being engaged in occupations according to their natures. To attain these goals, you advised us to distribute land to our members where they can live and be productive, where they can construct their houses and raise their families and at the same time have Kṛṣṇa in the center and perform all activities for Him.

Furthermore, as early as 1975 you saw the need to establish on each farm a *varṇāśrama* college for professional training. Unfortunately, today—almost thirty-five years later—we have done very little to establish our own Society according to your instructions.

It is our dream to fulfill this desire of yours, and therefore we are asking for your blessings. The world is facing its worst economic crisis since 1929. Forty years ago you predicted that the present artificial civilization has no future and is doomed to fail. We were very slow to understand the urgent need to implement your call for an alternative way of life. Fortunately, many of your disciples and followers are finally working to complete your mission. The present materialistic society is being defeated by the Lord’s *māyā,* and everyone can now clearly see the importance of your community projects for the welfare of all humankind. To give shelter to all kinds of people and act for their spiritual upliftment (*para-upakāra*) is the mission of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, and therefore the mission of your ISKCON movement.

On this auspicious occasion, we pledge to use all our energy, all our economic means, and all our skills to fulfill your holy mission. Being aware of your teachings that the final result depends on Śrī Kṛṣṇa, we will try to offer you this result by your next Vyāsa-pūjā, namely to establish some sample of your Society based on simply living and high thinking. Although we don’t have the qualification, we have full faith in your words, and we pray with eagerness for your causeless mercy.

Begging to remain in the service to your lotus feet,

Your servants at ISKCON Malaga, Spain.  
(written by Jīvatattva Dāsa)

## Manokvari

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

All glories to Śrī Guru and Gaurāṇga! All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Please accept our humble obeisances. Please kindly be merciful to us, your insignificant servants in West Papua, so that we will be able to remain on the path of *bhakti,* Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, I feel that I do not have enough words to glorify your greatness. You are most merciful. Just by having full faith in your guru and Kṛṣṇa, you took out of India the most valuable, incomparable wealth in the form of Kṛṣṇa consciousness and spread it to the Western countries, and then to the whole world.

You are the most beloved disciple of Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura. You proved Lord Caitanya’s prediction that the holy name of the Lord would be chanted in every village and town all over the world. And now His holy names are being vibrated here in West Papua, in the eastern part of Indonesia. You have also translated and published many spiritual literatures, such as *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* and *Bhagavad-gītā,* which help bring us closer to Kṛṣṇa.

You are the head of the Vaiṣṇava spiritual family known as ISKCON, which can bring unity to the whole human race. The whole world is indebted to Your Divine Grace. Even though you have left us physically, you are present through the teachings in your books. And now Lord Caitanya’s mission is being continued by your disciples through book distribution, *prasādam* distribution, the building of temples, and the establishment of various centers, schools, etc. These are all getting full attention from your disciples. But one mission of yours that has not yet gotten full attention is *varṇāśrama-dharma*.

At present, we here in Manokvari are learning to develop this mission by having a farmhouse and engaging in cow protection. In Manokvari, at the Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Manohara Āśrama, we have a small piece of land, about two hectares, and also three cows. In this way we are trying to develop *varṇāśrama-dharma*. We are trying to be self-sufficient by making our own coconut oil, planting vegetables, cooking with biogas, etc.

We pray that, by your kind mercy, we will be able to at least maintain what we have now and gradually improve our devotion to guru and Kṛṣṇa through *varṇāśrama-dharma*.

Your servants at the Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Manohara Mandir, Monokvari, Irian Jaya, West Papua, Indonesia.  
(written by Durāśaya Dāsa)

## Melbourne

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

We offer our most humble obeisances repeatedly at your lotus feet.

We are happy to report that your ISKCON family continues to grow here at the Melbourne temple. As a result of the four processes of book distribution, *prasādam* distribution, Deity worship, and *harināma-saṅkīrtana*, an increasing number of people in the wider community are embracing and appreciating the movement you established.

There is an undeniable potency in what you have created for us. The programs for preaching are very simple, but as you stated repeatedly, their success depends on our following the standard rules very seriously. You understood very clearly what we needed in order to become Kṛṣṇa conscious. We continue to be appreciative and amazed at how expertly you created your Society and its structured programs for bringing everyone to the platform of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Wherever we see pure devotional service being executed enthusiastically under your guidance, we can observe very positive and significant results. Our personal and collective success in ISKCON becomes consolidated when we stick sincerely to what you have given us.

With your deep understanding of the desires of the previous *ācāryas,* combined with your profound realizations of their teachings, you have kindly presented these conclusions to the world at large—this is your great gift to humanity. Many people all over the planet are appreciating this gift, and we hope that as time goes by your contribution will be appropriately recognized more universally.

We feel a great debt of gratitude for the opportunities you have created for us. As your servants here in Melbourne, we pray to be able to continue to serve you with enthusiasm, thus assisting you in your mission in whatever way we can.

Śrīla Prabhupāda-*kī jaya*!

Your servants at Mahāprabhu Mandir, Melbourne, Australia.  
(written by Prabhu Dasa)

## Miami

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet. Every year on the occasion of your Vyāsa-pūjā, when we celebrate your appearance in this mortal world, we have an opportunity to express and increase our appreciation of all that you have given us. Your gift is not only for those who understand your mission and are trying to serve it, but it is for the whole world.

Clearly, you have given us many temples, your transcendental literatures, the process of Deity worship, *prasādam,* and knowledge of the perfection of the human form of life. Your millions of servants and well-wishers gratefully acknowledge that these blessings are directly your selfless contribution to whoever will accept them. In your letters you always wrote, “Please accept my blessings.” You were begging us to accept your blessings in the form of everything you gave us to help us become Kṛṣṇa conscious. What you are not given due credit for are the long-range residual effects of your mission.

You injected human society with the medicine of devotional service and the chanting of the holy names of the Lord. As a result, innumerable temples have sprung up around the world, where devotional service is performed daily and the holy names are regularly chanted. Not only do those acts purify the performers, but they purify and change the whole world. For example, since you established the International Society for Krishna Consciousness in 1966, the world has witnessed a great increase in the number of vegetarians and those who aspire to become vegetarian. Animal slaughter goes on in the absence of mercy, and the last traces of mercy that still exist are set to decline throughout this age. And yet, the effects of Kali-yuga are being counteracted by Lord Caitanya’s *saṅkīrtana* movement. As the founder-*ācārya* of ISKCON, you have firmly established His movement through the International Society for Krishna Consciousness.

Even though this is clearly Kali-yuga, we can see positive changes, changes we never expected. Communism was a great thorn in the sides of those who wanted to practice religious life, but by the grace of Lord Caitanya, communism failed in the former USSR and Kṛṣṇa consciousness is becoming firmly established there. Even the world struggle for control of oil has started to lead to the development of alternative energy sources like solar power, wind, electric cars, and so on. This is contrary to the material trend of Kali-yuga, which is to increase sinful life more and more. You have taught us how greatly sinful it is to exploit the earth’s resources for sense gratification. To the degree that the population is sinful, the blessings that Lord Caitanya has given through you and your mission will not be accepted. Lord Caitanya’s *saṅkīrtana* movement is helping to hold back the bad effects of this Kali-yuga, and through His external energy He is mystically transforming the world in favor of genuine spiritual life.

You established the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement as a brahminical movement. You wanted to create a class of *brāhmaṇas,* or first-class men and women, who could show the world by example how to live in Kṛṣṇa consciousness. You are training us to be ideal by character, by religion, by behavior, and by education. You have told us that this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement will continue to increase for the next ten thousand years. You have also told us that if even one percent of the world becomes ideal men, the rest of the world will follow and will become peaceful by accepting the principles of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, even if they cannot immediately follow them.

Your sincere devotees all over the world are working hard to maintain and increase this movement. Here at ISKCON Miami, New Naimiṣāraṇya Forest, we are also working hard to propagate your mission. Over the years we have met many difficult challenges, and have several times faced the clear reality that we could lose our temple. But we have remained steadfast in our dedication to maintain your temple and service to our Deities, and gradually we are seeing some light at the end of the tunnel. In fact, we are confident that we have achieved some special blessing of the Lord as a result of not abandoning ship when times grew rough.

We fully expect that wonderful changes will continue to come about in the future. Our financial burden has decreased, and the temple’s relationship with the devotee community is improving. Overall, we are working to establish Kṛṣṇa consciousness in Miami, especially in Coconut Grove, so that it will become a Kṛṣṇa conscious city. If one percent of the world’s population becomes ideal, the rest of the world will follow. So if one percent of the people in Coconut Grove become devotees, the rest of the people here would likely strive to follow their example.

We hope that you were pleased with our special efforts to inundate Coconut Grove with love of Kṛṣṇa during our first Coconut Grove Kṛṣṇa Fest. Both temple devotees and congregational members were excited about being engaged in putting on this three-day festival during the same weekend we celebrated Lord Nityānanda’s appearance day. We even engaged a group of young-adult devotees from another temple. Some members of this ecstatic Gurukuli *bhajan* group said this was the best festival for Lord Nityānanda’s appearance that they had ever taken part in.

During your physical presence in this world, the devotees in every temple were eager to bring you new prospective disciples and news of their temple’s achievements. In the same way, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we aspire for the day when we will be able to offer you Coconut Grove as a Kṛṣṇa conscious city. If a modern American city were to become Kṛṣṇa conscious and peaceful, and could practically execute all the normal functions of a city, for sure the rest of the suffering world would watch and other cities would gradually develop in the same way.

In the *Caitanya-caritāmṛta* (*Ādi-līlā* 8.5), Śrīla Kṛṣṇadāsa Kavirāja writes,

*mūka kavitva kare yāṅ-sabāra smaraṇe*  
*paṅgu giri laṅghe, andha dekhe tārā-gaṇe*

**“By remembering the lotus feet of the Pañca-tattva, a dumb man can become a poet, a lame man can cross mountains, and a blind man can see the stars in the sky.”**

In other words, just by remembering Lord Caitanya and his associates, that which would normally be impossible will become possible. Of course, remembering Lord Caitanya means serving His mission, and serving His mission means that we must serve the order of his pure devotee. As the founder-*ācārya* of the International Society for Krishna Consciousness, you have established certain rules and standards for all who join your mission. You have commanded without exception that we all daily chant sixteen rounds of the Hare Kṛṣṇa mantra and that we abstain from four sinful acts: eating meat, fish, or eggs, having illicit sex, using any intoxicants, and gambling. You also insisted that we attend the morning and evening temple programs and work cooperatively to propagate this movement. By following these simple rules you gave us, we can accomplish the seemingly impossible.

We know that many of the devotees in our movement and associated with our Miami temple do not strictly follow all of the regulative principles or chant sixteen rounds of the Hare Kṛṣṇa mantra daily. Therefore we pray that you will continue to guide us and bless us to be able to follow the basic standards of Kṛṣṇa consciousness so that we can not only become pure devotees of Lord Kṛṣṇa ourselves but achieve our desire to one day offer you Coconut Grove as a Kṛṣṇa conscious city, complete with *gurukula* schools, *prasādam* restaurants, city government leaders who are devotees, citywide Kṛṣṇa conscious festivals, and an all-pervasive spiritual atmosphere that will inspire anyone who visits to want to know about Lord Kṛṣṇa.

Your humble servants at the ISKCON temple in Miami, Florida, USA.

## Montreal

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

It is a great pleasure and an honor for me that on this most auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā I can present this humble offering on behalf of your servants at the Montreal *yātrā*.

In the sixties, in the days when I began to seek illumination, it was a fashion to have a guru, and I wondered what a guru would be like. I could very easily have been cheated, because I had no clue what a great soul was. By Kṛṣṇa’s mercy, however, I did not have to go through the process of searching, testing, etc., nor was I fooled by anyone, because you came to me first. Thus in full satisfaction I witnessed a genuine guru in Your Divine Grace. We saw with our own eyes how you acted, spoke, thought, taught, wrote, preached, worshiped, etc. Indeed, we witnessed all the famous qualities of a Vaiṣṇava emanate from your good self. Like a touchstone, you changed so many rascals like me into devotees. You always put Kṛṣṇa in the center and did not take any personal credit for anything, and you made sure we acted in the same way. You showed us the real meaning of compassion by rescuing us from our miserable condition of life. You conquered the hearts of so many, and anyone who had the privilege of meeting you was transformed. The quest for the Absolute Truth ends with you; there is absolutely no need to go somewhere else. It starts and ends in appreciation of you. Anyone can perfect his or her life by serving you. It is only by unmotivated service that we can achieve your lotus feet. How could it be otherwise? You are never impressed by anything except by hard work in Kṛṣṇa consciousness. You are like a thunderbolt for those who take undue advantage of this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement, and you are soft like a rose for the sincere soul who wants to please you.

You once said, “If you are not able to increase what I have started, then at least maintain it. Above all, don’t spoil anything.”

You started this Montreal *yātrā* in 1967, and by your grace it is still going on. The devotional service all of us here engage in to maintain this center is performed in full knowledge that we are pleasing you, because we know that ISKCON Montreal is dear to your heart. You are pleased to see that a project you worked to establish is being taken care of nicely by your sincere devotees. Your pleasure alone is so rewarding that we don’t want anything else. Your joy is our joy, and your disappointment our fear. Even though we don’t feel qualified, by your mercy everything is possible. Therefore we are taking responsibility for your mission here in Montreal, and to please you we are ready to do the needful. We are uniting together and stand strong for you. We will face any difficulty and work it out to please you. We will take nice care of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Manohara, Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Madanamohana, Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi, and Śrī Śrī Jagannātha, Baladeva, and Subhadrā. We will keep on performing the *āratis,* going on *harināma,* distributing your transcendental books, and conducting the Sunday Feast, Ratha-yātrā, and all the other festivals you introduced. Here in Montreal there are so many opportunities for preaching, and by your grace we will do what is necessary to represent you in all instances. After all, ISKCON is not a social club but a preaching movement. Without you we are nothing, but with you standing by our side we are the best. We hope we will never forget to whom it all belongs.

Your humble servants in Montreal, Canada.  
(written by Anubhāva Dāsa)

## Mumbai (Juhu)

Our dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

On January 12, 2009, we performed a Bhūmi-pūjā and laid the foundation stone for the new building of the Bhaktivedanta Swami Mission School on your property in Sastri Nagar. We are ready to start the construction, but we have to wait for government permission. Please guide us to develop this project properly.

As we planned last year, this year we replaced the old *kalaśas* on top of the temple domes with new gilded ones. We are now designing the flagstaffs that will be placed on top of the domes. Your guidance is essential.

Your GBC body accepted our invitation to hold their general meeting in your “office,” and last October the meeting was successfully held here. We tried our best to serve the members, and they were so pleased with the service that they graciously accepted our invitation to meet here again in October of this year. Please give us the intelligence to serve them better this year.

Last October, during the GBC meeting, your dear servant His Holiness Jayapatāka Swami underwent a serious health problem. We did our best to serve him with all the facilities at our disposal. He is recuperating well, and we are sure this is pleasing to Your Divine Grace.

Among all temples in the world, ISKCON Juhu came in second place in 2008 in your transcendental book distribution program. This year we are increasing our book distribution by engaging the congregational devotees. We seek your blessings so that we may emerge as the top distributor this year.

One of your dearest devotees in Mumbai, Śrī P.L. Sethiji, passed away on February 16, 2009. During his final days, the devotees performed continuous *kīrtana* at his bedside, and everybody devotedly attended the funeral. We hope you are pleased with our service to Śrī Sethiji.

We are establishing a kitchen on Hare Krishna Land for the midday meal program. Please give us the intelligence to understand your mood for this *prasādam* distribution, and guide us how to execute it.

During his recent visit, His Holiness Girirāja Swami mentioned that the guestrooms in the Heaven on Earth building are nice. He commented that the old guesthouse rooms could be renovated to match the new ones. We are therefore renovating the rooms in the old guesthouse. We hope they are befitting the standard of your “office.”

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for giving us this rare opportunity to render service at the lotus feet of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Rāsabiharījī.

Your insignificant servants at ISKCON Juhu, Mumbai, India.

## Nagpur

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace on the glorious occasion of your auspicious Vyāsa-pūjā!

We dedicate this day to remembering you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, but actually we think of you and meditate on your glorious life every single day because without your teachings, where would we be? The chaos in the world today is beyond the ability of most people to handle well, if at all. Negative emotions such as fear, anger, anxiety, and distress decrease people’s ability to solve problems and weaken their resilience, leading to the use of drugs and alcohol to mitigate the hopelessness of life. Their only hope is Kṛṣṇa consciousness, which you worked so hard to give everyone in the world.

Without ever meeting you personally, we have grown to love you so much. We have read books by other historical figures, but there is certainly no feeling for the authors. We respond to you because of your causeless mercy upon those who are suffering in this material world, because you showed us the way, took us out of our mediocre lives, and gave us a road map to discover what true living really means. You taught us how to achieve the ultimate goal of life, how to go where we truly belong, back home, back to Godhead.

We had so much confusion about God. Who is God? Where is God? Does He really exist? In no uncertain terms, you disclosed the identity of God and revealed it to the general populace. “Kṛṣṇa is God,” you said, “the Supreme Personality of Godhead. This is what He looks like, these are His activities, this is where He lives.” You validated all your statements with proof from the ancient scriptures of India. You gave us the answers to all our questions.

Although there were so many translations of the *Bhagavad-gītā,* reading them simply confused people more and more, whereas reading your *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is* leaves no doubt in anyone’s mind about the identity of God, our own identity, and our relationship with Him. You gave us the chanting of the Hare Kṛṣṇa mantra and gorgeous Deities in beautiful temples, the opulent worship of whom had never before been seen in the world. You made the scriptures accessible to us all, in many languages, transporting us to the spiritual world, a world we never even knew existed. You taught us to develop a higher taste and that food offered to Kṛṣṇa was not just food but *prasādam,* the mercy of the Lord.

On your departure, you left behind your beloved disciples to carry on your work. It is said that one who serves the guru’s message really worships him, and this we see in your disciples. They have not only maintained ISKCON but increased it by their commitment and sustained efforts. They are serving you so nicely by broadcasting Lord Caitanya’s message all over the world. Because of their sincere hard work, many, many others have also been inspired to become part of the tapestry of ISKCON.

This year, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we experienced a very happy event. There was a new temple opening at Aravade, Maharashtra, the birthplace of your dear disciple His Holiness Lokanāth Swami, our Guru Mahārāja. It is a magnificent temple, constructed with great care and love. Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gopāla are very, very beautiful, and very merciful, too. The opening was attended by many of Mahārāja’s disciples who came from all countries of the world. Many of Mahārāja’s godbrothers and godsisters also attended the various functions. They all appreciated the gorgeous temple. It was so heart-warming to hear them all praising Mahārāja and glorifying him for his lifetime’s work. Mahārāja said, “It is just a small glimpse of the spiritual world.” Do you remember that you wanted him to make everyone in his village Kṛṣṇa conscious so that they would not all run to the cities? Well, this temple will now be the mainstay of village life there.

What greater fortune could there be than to be given the gift of Kṛṣṇa consciousness by a pure devotee? And for that, we have you to thank. Because of you we are all the most fortunate souls—we have knowledge of Kṛṣṇa. This is real knowledge, and we do not have to look elsewhere anymore.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we hear and read of so many great things being accomplished by all the ISKCON centers worldwide. So far, we have not done anything glorious or outstanding. We have still to transcend our limitations to achieve a higher goal. We do not ask for greatness, but please shower us with blessings so that we may cooperate to serve our spiritual master and Your Divine Grace by propagating the message of Lord Caitanya, the message of Godhead.

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda! All glories to Śrī Guru and ŚrīGaurāṅga!

Your humble servants at the Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gopīnātha Temple in Nagpur, India.

## New Biharvan

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Please accept our humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet, the only comforting place in this great forest of the material world.

The city of Boise has many hundreds of trees (*boise* is French for “trees”) and a river running through it, and lately we can also see a forest of relatively tall office buildings. The city is surrounded by mountains and desert. Several times it has been rated one of the top fifty American cities in which to live. Boiseans feel very fortunate to live here, not realizing the miseries that lie in their path. In actuality, Boise, like any other modern city, very much resembles the forest described in chapters 13 and 14 of the Fifth Canto of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* during talks between King Rahūgaṇa and Jaḍa Bharata.

In the summary to chapter 13 you write:

The *brāhmaṇa* Jaḍa Bharata became very kind to King Rahūgaṇa, and to disassociate him from the material world, he spoke figuratively of the forest of the material world. He explained that this material world is like a great forest in which one becomes entangled due to association with material life.

In your purport to verse 1 of chapter 14, you write:

Since the material world is compared herein to a forest, it may be argued that in Kali-yuga modern civilization is mainly situated in the cities. A great city, however, is like a great forest. Actually city life is more dangerous than life in the forest. If one enters an unknown city without friend or shelter, living in that city is more difficult than living in a forest. There are many big cities all over the surface of the globe, and wherever one looks he sees the struggle for existence going on twenty-four hours a day. People rush about in cars going seventy and eighty miles an hour, constantly coming and going, and this sets the scene of the great struggle for existence. One has to rise early in the morning and travel in that car at breakneck speed. There is always the danger of an accident, and one has to take great care. In his automobile, the living entity is full of anxieties, and his struggle is not at all auspicious.”

We in ISKCON Boise, New Biharvan, are a community of *gṛhastha* devotees struggling in the material forest and living the scenario presented by you. The community has seen many tribulations during the last year as the world becomes more and more hellish and the fires of recession, unemployment, and layoffs decimates the congregation and temple devotees.

The ray of sunshine in the Boise forest is Your Divine Grace. Just as the *brāhmaṇa* Jaḍa Bharata became very kind to King Rahūgaṇa and spoke to him to disassociate him from the material world, you speak to us every day with your powerful purports to disassociate us from this material world. You not only point out the problems but very kindly and decisively give us the solution. In your summary of chapter 14, “The Material World as the Great Forest of Enjoyment,” you write, “Without the protection of an all-merciful Vaiṣṇava. the conditioned soul cannot get out of the clutches of *māyā.*” For us on this earth planet, you are that all-merciful Vaiṣṇava who can protect us and get us out of *māyā*. You are the *sena-pati* in Lord Caitanya’s army who are saving lost souls from the terrorism of *māyā*.

Thus in the purport to verse 1 of chapter 14 you write:

The most important information in this verse is *hari-guru-caraṇa-aravinda-madhukara-anupādavīm*. In this material world the conditioned souls are baffled by their activities, and sometimes they are relieved after great difficulty. On the whole the conditioned soul is never happy. He simply struggles for existence. Actually his only business is to accept the spiritual master, the guru, and through him he must accept the lotus feet or the Lord. This is explained by Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu: *guru-kṛṣṇa-prasāde pāya bhakti-latā-bīja.* People struggling for existence in the forests or cities of the material world are not actually enjoying life. They are simply suffering different pains and pleasures, generally pains that are always inauspicious. They try to gain release from these pains, but they cannot due to ignorance. For them it is stated in the *Vedas: tad-vijñānārthaṁ sa gurum evābhigacchet*. When the living entity is lost in the forest of the material world, in the struggle for existence, his first business is to find a bona fide guru who is always engaged at the lotus feet of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Viṣṇu. After all, if he is at all eager to be relieved of the struggle for existence, he must find a bona fide guru and take instructions at his lotus feet. In this way he can get out of the struggle.

You further write:

This Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement has been started to give an opportunity to all the members of human society, who are misled by foolish leaders. No one can get out of this struggle for existence, which is full of miseries, without accepting a pure devotee of the Lord. The material attempt changes from one position to another, and no one actually gains relief from the struggle for existence. The only resort is the lotus feet of a bona fide spiritual master, and, through him, the lotus feet of the Lord.

Similarly, we struggle. However, we feel very fortunate to have the shelter of your exalted self, your books, your purports in them, the beautiful Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Baṅkebihārī, your disciples, and other senior Vaiṣṇavas who come to our forest distributing their mercy. In a small way, the devotees in New Biharvan also try to give other Boiseans an opportunity to taste your greatness through the Deities, your books, festivals, and *prasādam*.

Please keep us under the cooling shade of your most divine lotus feet.

The devotees at New Biharvan, Boise, Idaho, USA.  
(written by Anantarūpa Dāsa)

## New Dvārakā

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your most merciful lotus feet. We pray that you will accept this feeble attempt to glorify you on this auspicious day of your appearance.

In *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* (3.25.20) Lord Kaplia states:

*prasaṅgam ajaraṁ pāśam ātmanaḥ kavayo viduḥ*  
*sa eva sādhuṣu kṛto mokṣa-dvāram apāvṛtam*

**“Every learned man knows very well that attachment for the material is the greatest entanglement of the spirit soul. But that same attachment, when applied to the self-realized devotees, opens the door of liberation.”**

Over the years, by your association, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are gradually recognizing how deeply attached we are to this material existence. We don’t want to say that we understand, because if we understood the consequences we would act upon that understanding. That has not been our fortune; rather, we have been chewing the chewed, time and time again, like a moth repeatedly bouncing off a light.

*kṛṣṇa bhuli’ sei jīva anādi-bahirmukha*  
*ataeva māyā tāre deya saṁsāra-duḥkha*

**“Forgetting Kṛṣṇa, the living entity has been attracted by the external feature from time immemorial. Therefore the illusory energy (*****māyā*****) gives him all kinds of misery in his material existence.”**

Just like a moth attracted to a light, we are insanely attracted to *māyā,* but our only reward is a lot of misery and, as with the moth, an uneventful death.

You stated in a *Bhagavad-gītā* lecture in London in 1973 that the goal of human life is to be immortal. This is not some fantasy or movie-superhero idea, where you get stuck here in the material world and live a lonely life, watching everyone around you dying. Even the scientists have no idea what immortality means or how to achieve it.

*Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* states, *mahat-sevāṁ dvāram āhur vimuktes.* The door to this knowledge and existence is opened by serving the self-realized soul. Serving means first hearing. The *Bhagavad-gītā* states, *vyāsa-prasādāc chrutavān etad guhyam ahaṁ param*. Sañjaya states that it is by the mercy of Vyāsa that he could understand the Supreme Personality of Godhead. One has to understand Kṛṣṇa through the medium of one who knows Him. To understand the eternal through a medium is still a direct experience. You are that bona fide medium, Śrīla Prabhupāda, as evidenced by your words and deeds.

The Lord states in the *Bhagavad-gītā*(18.57), *cetasā sarva-karmāṇi mayi sannyasya mat-paraḥ.* You only depended on Kṛṣṇa, working under His protection, always conscious of Him; therefore you were never unduly anxious in executing your mission of spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness. We, on the other hand, are always anxious because of being in the bodily concept of life.

You stated in a letter in 1975:

Those who are not sober cannot understand how the spirit soul is changing from one body to another, that the modern education is producing only the *adhīra* class who are neither sober nor educated.

You worked tirelessly to give the world your books and show the proper example, but we are so unfortunate that we haven’t taken your teachings and example seriously. We are so condemned! But in another letter in 1970 you stated:

Because the Vaiṣṇavas are there, there is ample chance for the demons to be blessed by the devotees. The devotees of the Lord are so powerful that each and every one of them has the power of delivering many demons, even those condemned by the Lord.

Because you are such a Vaiṣṇava, we feel hopeful that you will feel compassion upon our lowly selves. We have no claim to your mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda, but the preaching work of the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement is specifically meant for delivering the most fallen. You said that because this is the Age of Kali people are not very much eager to accept genuine things. That is true of us, which makes us disappointed in ourselves, for you are giving the most genuine process, *kṛṣṇa-bhakti*. Yet we pray for your compassion and the strength to endure whatever austerities it may take to attract your attention.

For yourself you ask nothing except that we assist you in fulfilling the desire of your guru, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura, to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness around the world. You said we would be successful if we didn’t slacken off or neglect our “routine work,” namely, rising early, cleansing, chanting sixteen rounds daily, and performing street *saṅkīrtana*. Even in that you have been so kind, simplifying it for us. “Regarding preaching work, if you simply reproduce verbatim the purports which I have given in *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* and chant Hare Kṛṣṇa with ecstasy, that will be sufficient for your preaching.” Please bless us to be one of the hundreds of bona fide students you said you required in the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement.

Prahlāda Mahārāja says, *śoce tato vimukha-cetasa indriyārtha-māyā-sukhāya bharam udvahato vimūḍhān.* The devotee’s anxiety is that the whole world is in need of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. You said to one disciple that by Kṛṣṇa’s grace your anxiety was televised to him in his dream. Please use whatever means are necessary, Śrīla Prabhupāda, to make it clear to our dull heads.

The Lord says in the *Bhagavad-gītā*(18.67):

*idaṁ te nātapaskāya nābhaktāya kadācana*  
*na cāśuśrūṣave vācyaṁ na ca māṁ yo 'bhyasūyati*

**“This confidential knowledge may never be explained to those who are not austere, or devoted, or engaged in devotional service, nor to one who is envious of Me.”**

We may not be austere, we may lack devotion, and we may even perform our service mechanically, but we have firm faith in your message that Śrī Kṛṣṇa is our Lord and best friend. You once said, “Our business is to glorify Kṛṣṇa as the Lord of Vṛndāvana and to popularize Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s Hare Kṛṣṇa movement.” Please give us your mercy so that we may be attached only to this business.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, *dayā koro more.*

Your humble servants in New Dvārakā, Los Angeles, California, USA.  
(written by Naikātmā Dāsa)

## New Gokula (Australia)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my humble obeisances.

May your glorious deeds be resounded in all directions, for this day marks the only really significant milestone in modern history. Forget independence day, country-founding day, the Queen’s birthday, mother’s day, father’s day, Christmas, and Easter. Just farcical.

May the exalted qualities of your spotless character be studied and appreciated. A pure devotee’s qualities set the standard for human behavior.

May honest souls in this world offer their heartfelt gratitude for your unmatchable contribution to peace in the world.

And may we always remain grateful to be among the many recipients of your kind blessings.

Without your continuing intervention, how could we possibly, on our own, free ourselves from our karmic destinies?

Together we are grappling to imbibe the serious lessons on Kṛṣṇa conscious practices revealed in the Bhaktivedanta purports and subsequently revealed to us in the unfolding process of the spiritual journey.

Today we few devotees here in the New Gokula farming community bow down at your beautiful lotus feet, feeling awed by the import of this occasion, the 113th anniversary of your divine appearance. Please allow our small cup of gratitude to swell into a flowing stream of lifetime service to your transcendental ISKCON.

Your servants at the New Gokula Farm, Millfield, Australia.  
(written by Kāliya-Kṛṣṇa Dāsa)

## New Govardhana (Australia)

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,
